Mwamulizo 1

U wusimulile wo wuwumbwe we Yinze

1 Ku mwamulizo Mulungu yawumba u wulanga ne yinze.
2 Yinze yali yisina futa yo yose, hamba kuwa na cinhu co cose. Ne citi cali cili hacanyha, civigubicize ivisolo vye malenga, no Muhe wo Mulungu wali watulile mucanyha ye malenga.
3 U Mulungu yakatya, “Wuwe wuzelu, nawo wuzelu wukawa.
4 U Mulungu yakawuwona wuzelu vyono wuswanu muno muno. Holeka Mulungu yakawa kuwutemanula u wuzelu ne citi,
5 no wuzelu yakawucema ‘Misi’ naco citi yakacicema ‘Cilo.’ ” Likaswa, wukela, lizuwa lilongozi.

6 U Mulungu yakatya, “Wuwe mwanya haciligati ye malenga, ho wugatemanule malenga galawe hejete.”
7 Holeka Mulungu yakawunoza mwanya wuwo, wukagatemanula i malenga gono gali mucanyha yo mwanya wuwo ne malenga gono gali hasi yayo, nayo vyo yikawa nhavivyo.
8 Nayo Mulungu yakawucema wuwo mwanya, “Wulanga.” Likaswa, wukela, lizuwa lye kejete.
9 U Mulungu yakatya, “I malenga gono gali hasi yo wulanga gehangulizize hamonga, hakavu ho hasume kwijela.” Nayo vyo yikawa nhavivyo.
10 Nayo Mulungu yakahacema baho i hakavu, “Yisi” na agalya malenga gono gakehanguliziza hamonga, yakagacema, “Yilenga.” U Mulungu yakaziwona zizo zose vyono swanu muno muno.
11 Mulungu yakatya, “Yisi yicindye mibici ye mawala gose, mibici yono yikulela, ne mibici yono yikulela mikalo yono yina ne zimbeyu mugati yayo.” Nayo vyo yikawa nhavivyo.
12 Yinze yikawa kucindya mawala gose ge vihadikwa vyose, vihadikwa vyono vilelaga zimbeyu, ne vihadikwa vyono vina ne zimbeyu zayo mugati yayo. U Mulungu yakaviwona vivyo vyose vyono viswanu muno muno.
13 Likaswa, wukela, lizuwa lye kadatu.

14 U Mulungu yakatya, “Mamuli gejelaje mu mwanya ho gavitemanule misi ne cilo, ho gasume kuwulajila u mwamulizo we lizuwa, ne madaha, ne myaka, ne mapango.
15 Ne mamuli gago ho gasume kumulika no kuyimulicila yinze.” Nayo vyo yikawa nhavivyo.
16 Hanyuma u Mulungu yakawa kuganoza mamuli mejete mawaha, ilimuli liwaha lisume kutema mu mapango ge misi, ne limuli lidodo, lisume kutema mu mapango ge cilo, nayo Mulungu kanyhi yakawa kunoza ne zinyelezi.
17 Mulungu yakawa kugawika mu mwanya i mamuli gago ho gasume kuyimulicila i yinze,
18 ho gasume kutema ku misi na ku cilo, kanyhi ho gasume kuvitemanula u wuzelu ne citi. Mulungu yakaviwona vivyo vyose vyono viswanu muno muno.
19 Likaswa, wukela, lizuwa lye kane.

20 U Mulungu yakatya, “I malenga gameme viwumbwa vyose vyumi, ne zindeje nazo ziguluce mu mwanya mucanyha.”
21 U Mulungu yakawa kuwumba ko winji muno muno, viwumbwa viwaha vyo kogofya vya mwi'lenga, hamonga ne viwumbwa vinji vyumi vye mawala gose, vya mwi'lenga, vyono vikalaga mumo, nayo yakawa kuziwumba ne zindeje ze mawala gose. U Mulungu yakaviwona vivyo vyose vyono viswanu muno muno.
22 U Mulungu yakavilugutiza, yakutya, “Mwilele monjelezece, mulimemye lilenga, i zindeje nazo zonjelezece muno muno mono yinze.”
23 Likaswa wukela, lizuwa lye 'hano.
24 U Mulungu yakatya, “Yisi yilele i mawala gose ge vilimu vyumi, ge zinimu zo kufugwa, ne vilimu vyose vyono vitambalaga hasi, sunga ne vilimu vya mu mbago.” Nayo vyo yikawa nhavivyo.
25 Hodu, Mulungu yakawa kuganoza i malimu ga mu mbago ge mawala gose, yakanoza kanyhi ne zimitugo ze mawala gose, hamonga ne viwumbwa vyose vye mawala gose vyono vitambalaga hasi. U Mulungu yakaziwona zizo zose vyono swanu muno muno.

26 Hanyuma u Mulungu yakatya, “Ale, cimunoze munhu ku hwani yetu, we futa yetu, yateme mu zisomba zose, ne zindeje zose zono zikwanga mucanyha, hamonga ne zimitugo zose, ne yinze yose, ne viwumbwa vyose vyono vitambalaga hasi.”
27 Mulungu yakawa kumuwumba u munhu ku hwani yakwe, ku hwani yakwe yakamuwumba. U mulume no mucekulu yawawumba.
28 U Mulungu yakawalugutiza, yakutya, “Mwilele mwinjipe muno muno, no kuyimemya yinze, no kuyinoza yinze yimuhulicenyi nyenye. Kanyhi muteme mu zisomba ne zindeje zose, na muli viwumbwa vyose vyumi vyono vili mono yinze.
29 Nane nghumupelenyi i mawala gose ge vilimwa, vyono vilelaga zimbeyu vyono vili mono'nze yiwe ndigwa yenyu, ne vilimwa vyose vye mawala gose vyono vilelaga mikalo yono yina ne zimbeyu zayo mugati yavyo.
30 Ninga i vilimu, hamonga ne zindeje zono zikwanga mucanyha, sunga ne viwumbwa vyose vyono vitambalaga hasi, hamonga ne viwumbwa vyose vyono vina wumi, avyo vyose nivipela i mahamha ge vilimwa vyose, yiwe yo ndigwa yavyo.” Nayo vyo yikawa nhavivyo.
31 Mulungu yakaviwona vinhu vyose vyono yaviwumbile vyono viswanu muno muno, likaswa, wukela, lizuwa lyo mutandatu.


Mwamulizo 2

1 Ale, u wulanga ne yinze, hamonga ne vinhu vyose vyono vili mumo, vikawa vyakondya.
2 Lyeza kufika ilizuwa lyo mupunghati, u Mulungu nayo, yakawa yayikondya i milimo yono yetumaga, na mwi'zuwa lilyo lyo mupunghati, yakawa kuceha.
3 Lizuwa lilyo lyo mupunghati, Mulungu yakalilugutiza no kulisiwanula, vyono lyo lizuwa lyono mwene yacehela mu wulawa mu milimo yakwe yose yo kuwumba.
4 Na vyo yinha, u wulanga ne yinze vyo vyawumbwa.

U mugunda wa Adeni

Na mwi'zuwa lono u Mulungu yawumbila u wulanga ne yinze,
5 i yinze yali yisina vilimwa vyo vyose, hamba mibici yali yikali sinacindika, hambi Mulungu, u Muwaha Mulungu, nha yali yakali sinatonyeza mvula mu yinze, hamba munhu wo kuyilima, hali hakali hasina.
6 Hamba nhavivyo, imalenga gakalawa hasi nha mivwilo no kuwuloweka muhangha wose.
7 Baho u Mulungu, yakasola lilongo, yakamuwumba munhu, yakawa kumuputila u muhe wo wumi mu mhula, nayo munhu yuyo yakawa ciwumbwa cumi.
8 Hanyuma u Mulungu, yakahadika mugunda kuko ku Adeni, ku lubavu lwa kucilima, yakawa kumuwika mumo mu mugunda u munhu yuyo mono yamuwumba.
9 Mulungu yakacindya mu yisi yose, i mawala gose ge mibici miswanu, yono yikulela mikalo yono yikwiliye so ndigwa ye wanhu. Ninga haciligati yo mugunda wuwo, hali howuli u mubici wo wumi, kanyhi no mubici wo kumanyila i ziswanu ne zimbi.

10 Baho ha Adeni, hali holili likolongo lyono lyahulumaga no kuwumwajila malenga u mugunda wuwo, na kanyhi ku wulawa mu Adeni, likolongo lilyo likegawa likawa makolongo mane.
11 Likolongo lilongozi, likucemwa Pishoni, ne likolongo lilyo liyipilime yisi yose ya Havila, kuko kono zijelaga zizahabu.
12 Ne zahabu ye yisi yiyo, yo zahabu yono swanu muno. Na kuko, kanyhi ko gakuwa kwijela ne mafuta ge mhunjile swanu, gono gakuwa kucemwa bedola, sunga ne mabwe maswanu go wuguzi wukamu muno.
13 Likolongo lye kejete likucemwa Gihoni, ne likolongo lilyo liyipilime yisi yose ya Kushi.
14 Ne likolongo lye kadatu likucemwa Tigilisi, nalyo likalaga likuhuluma ku lubavu lwa kucilima ye yisi ya Ashuli, ne likolongo lye kane, likucemwa Fulata.

15 Hodu, u Mulungu yakamusola u munhu yuyo we cilume, yakamuwika mumo mu mugunda wa Adeni, ho yawulime no kuwulanjilila.
16 Mulungu, u Muwaha Mulungu, yakamulajiliza u munhu yuyo mulume, yakatya, “I mana ge mibici yose yono yili mu mugunda, kosuma kulya,
17 langa i mana go mubici wo kuzimanyila i ziswanu ne zimbi, wulece kulya hamba hadodo, wumanye vyono, ilizuwa lyono wulalila i mana gayo, hodu kolofwa.”
18 Hanyuma u Mulungu yakatya, “Si viswanu munhu yuyu we cilume yawe yali wene du! Ciba nimunozeze muyagwe, u mutazi mono komukwila.”
19 Holeka mu wulawa mwi'longo, u Muwaha Mulungu yaviwumba i vilimu vyose, hamonga ne zindeje zose zono zikwanga mucanyha, hanyuma yakawa kuvileta vyose kuli mulume yuyo, ho yawone kovicemela matagwaci, ne matagwa gono yavicemela viwumbwa vivyo, gago go gakawa i matagwa gavyo du.
20 Ale, u mulume yayo yakawa kuvicemela matagwa i vilimu vyose vyono vifugwaga, ne vilimu vya mu mbago, sunga ne zindeje zose. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, siyejela yunji mono yakawa mutazi mono yamukwiliye.

21 Holeka u Muwaha Mulungu yakawa kumupela u mulume yuyo nhongo nyinji muno muno. Yakawa kugona nhongo nzito. Na lono yali yali mu nhongo, u Mulungu yakamusopola u mulume yuyo lubavu lwakwe lumonga, ne hanhu hayo, yakawa kuhagubika ne nyama.
22 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu, yakalusola lubavu lulo, lono yalusopola ko mulume, yakamunoza mucekulu, hanyuma, yakawa kumuleta kuli mulume yuyo.
23 Nayo mulume yuyo yakatya,

“Hee, alu langa!

Ayu, lye fupa lya mu mafupa gangu,

kanyhi wo muwili wono wulawile mu muwili wangu,

holeka ayu kocemwa, ‘Mucekulu,’

ku soko vyono kasolwa muli mulume.”

24 Na ayi yo soko yono yikuleka, u mulume yawalece u sogwe no nyina, no kwilunza no mugolece wakwe, nawo wakuwa muwili wumonga.
25 Nawo wose, vyono wejete u mulume no mugolece wakwe, wali wali cipwi, ninga hamba siwawonaga cevu yaye.


Mwamulizo 3

U wu wula cihuliko wo munhu

1 Muli vilimu vyose vya mu mbago vyono u Mulungu yaviwumba, i nzoka yikawa yo sugu muno si nhe vilimu vyose. Holeka yikamulonjela u mucekulu yikamutya, “Ati, u Mulungu vyo yatya hongo, mulece kulya i mana ge mibici yose yono yili mu busitani?”
2 Nayo u mucekulu yakayipitula inzoka yakatya, “Aa, ng'o, cosuma kulya i mana ge mibici yose ya mu busitani,
3 langa hono si mana go mubici wono wuli haciligati ye busitani du, gago langa go Mulungu yacilajiliza vyono, cilece kulya, hamba kugapamiza kose, na hono cijelele du, cofwa.”
4 Inzoka nayo yikamulonjela u mucekulu, yikatya, “Waco, gwe, Mbeka hamba si mofwa ng'o.
5 U Mulungu yazilonga zizo, ku soko yali yamanyile vyono, hono mulye i mana go mubici wuwo, mokwenzelulwa meso genyu, nanye mowa mwehwana no mweneco Mulungu, mukuzimanya iziswanu ne zimbi.”

6 Nayo mucekulu yaza kuwulanga wuwo mubici, yakawuwona vyono wo mubici wono wukwendeza ha wulangwe. Sunga ne mana gayo, maswanu muno ku wulye. Kanyhi yakehwesa nha vyono, kumbe so mubici wuwo, kosuma kutuga mahala. Hodu yakawa kuhepa i mana go mubici wuwo, yakawa kulya, yakamupela no mulume wakwe, nayo nhavivyo, yakalya.
7 Nawo wali weza kulya, lo lulolulo i meso gawo gakenzelulwa wose vyono wejete, nawo wakawa kwimanya vyono wose wali cipwi. Hobaho wakawa kugahoneleza i mahamha go mukuyu, wakenozeza yikawa yo myenda yo kwitunga no kwihundiciziza.

8 Na mu cinyamihe cico, wakahulika lizi lyo Mulungu yakujenda mu mugunda, u mulume yuyo hamonga no mugolece wakwe, wose wakehundika mu mibici ya mu busitani, ati u Muwaha Mulungu ho yalece kuwawona.
9 U Muwaha Mulungu nayo yakawa kumucema u mulume, yakumutya, “Alo wuli hayi?”
10 Nayo mulume yuyo yakapitula yakatya, “Nilihulika lizi lyako mu busitani, nane niwa kogopa, niwa kwihundika, vyono ane nili cipwi.”
11 Nayo Mulungu yakamuwuza yakutya, “Alo nani mono yakulonjeye vyono agwe wuli cipwi? Wule, hamba kulya lo i mana go mubici wono nakulajiliza nghatya wulece kugalya?”
12 Nayo mulume yuyo yakapitula yakatya, “U mucekulu mono wamhela ciwezane nayo, yuyo yo yamhelile likalo lilyo, nane niwa kulilya.”
13 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakamuwuza yuyo mucekulu, yakamutya, “Alu 'gwe, nghanici yiyi lu yono witumile?” Nayo mucekulu yakapitula yakatya, “Inzoka yo yinghonjile, nane niwa kulya.”

Wuling'anize wo Mulungu

14 Holeka u Muwaha Mulungu yakawa kumitya i nzoka,

“Ku soko ye nghani yiyi yono wunozize,

wakondya kulijitwa si nhe vilimu vyose vyono vikuwa kufugwa,

kanyhi si nhe vilimu vyose vya mu mbago.

Kanyhi ku wulawa lulu,

kowa kujendela'nda,

na kanyhi kowa kwikala wukubwiga

manghundi du mu vilo vyako vyose vyo wikale wako.

15 Nane nowika wisule hagati yako no mucekulu yuyu,

na hagati yo lulelo lwako no lulelo lwakwe,

wowa wali wulugu.

Lolokutijinha u mutwe,

nagwe kolomuluma mu cising'anilo.”

16 Mulungu yakamulonjela nayo mucekulu, yakamutya,

“Agwe nagwe, nokuciziliziza i magaziko mu wudinghule wako we mida,

na kanyhi nokuciziliziza no wusungu lono wukusawa mwana.

Kosawa wana ko wusungu wuwaha muno,

ninga hamba nhavivyo,

kowa kwikala wukumusulumila u mulume wako,

nayo kowa yakutemile.”

17 Hanyuma yayo u Mulungu

yakamulonjela u mulume yuyo, yakamutya,

“Alu agwe nagwe,

vyono mwilumbaniza no mugolece wako,

nagwe kuwa kulya i mana go mubici

wono nakulajiliza vyono Wulece kugalya,

alu vyono wakondya kwituma zono zinha zizo,

ale, yisi yilijitwa ku soko yiyo.

Na ku wulawa lulu,

yowa yikunojeye witume milimo kwe nhuguti,

na kwe vilungo vyako vyose,

ho witujile ndigwa yono yokukwila

mu vilo vyako vyose vyo wikale wako.

18 Nayo yisi yocindya mabijili hamonga ne mamivya,

nagwe kowa kulya i mikalo ya mu mbago.

19 Kanyhi kowa kulya vya mu nhuguti yako,

sunga kolo weza kuwuwuyila u muhangha,

hambi nha mo walawa mumo,

agwe nha wuli manghundi,

holeka kowuya kanyhi mu manghundi.”

20 Adamu yakamucemela u mugolece wakwe litagwa lya “Hawa” hambi nha yo nyina we womi wose.
21 Nayo Mulungu yakawanozeza Adamu hamonga no mugolece wakwe, vivwalo vye zinguwo, yakawa kuwavwika.

Adamu na Hawa wakuwingwa wawuce mu Mugunda

22 Hanyuma u Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Alu Adamu, yaweza nha muyagwe na sese, hambi nha yakondya kuzimanya iziswanu ne zimbi, holeka yilece kuwa na lunji, yasole i mana go mubici wo wumi yalye no kwikala mumi cibitilila!”
23 Holeka u Muwaha Mulungu yakamuwinjila kunze yo mugunda wa Adeni yuyo Adamu, yabite yakawulime u muhangha mono yasolwa.
24 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yali yeza kukondya kumulavyaniza kunze ye mugunda, hodu, yakawa kuviwika viwumbwa vyono vikucemwa makelubi ku lubavu lwa kucilima yo mugunda wa Adeni, hamonga no mumaje wo moto, wono wupitukaga kose kose, so kuyikaliza inzila yono yibitile ku mubici wo wumi.


Mwamulizo 4

Kayini na Habeli

1 Nayo Adamu yakamumanya u mugolece wakwe Hawa, yakatuga 'nda, yakamulela Kayini. Nayo Hawa yakatya, “Ane namutuga mwana we cilume ko wutazi wo Muwaha Mulungu.”
2 Hanyuma kanyhi yakawa kumulela Habeli mwanakwawe. Habeli yali mudima ngholo, na Kayini nayo yali mulimi wa mugunda.
3 Hanyuma ya vilo vicefu, Kayini yakamulavizya Mulungu vilavywa ku wulawa mu vilimwa vya mu mugunda wakwe.
4 Habeli nayo, yakamulavizya Mulungu zinyana ze zingholo zinongozi kulelwa, za mwi'tewa lyakwe, zono zijinile viswanu muno muno. Nayo Muwaha Mulungu, yakendezwa muno muno na Habeli, hamonga ne vilavywa vyakwe.
5 Ninga u Mulungu siyendezwa hamba hadodo na Kayini, hamonga ne vilavywa vyakwe. Hodu, nayo Kayini yali yeza kuziwona zizo, yakawa kukolwa ne likuwo muno muno, no wusu wakwe wukakununghala muno muno, ku soko ya lilyo likuwo lyakwe.
6 U Muwaha Mulungu yakawa kumutya Kayini, “Kayini, alu nhawule wakolwa ne likuwo, sunga no wusu wako wukununghaye nhavivyo?
7 Alu, wule, si yili mbeka vyono nagwe wali wuvumilwe nhavivyo, hono wali witume viswanu? Ninga alu vyono witumile vibi, holeka u wubi wehwana lilimu, wikaye ha mulango wako wukukusulumila wukuhawanje, ninga yikunojeye agwe wuwusumize.”

8 Hanyuma yayo, Kayini yakamulonjela u mwanakwawe Habeli yakatya, “Gwe mwanakwetu, ale cibite cikatembele mu mawimbi.” Nawo lono wali wali kuko mu mawimbi, hodu Kayini yakawa kumuhungucila u mwanakwawe Habeli, yakawa kumuwulaga.
9 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakamuwuza Kayini yakutya, “Alu Kayini, u mwanakwenyu Habeli yali hayi lu?” Mwene Kayini yakapitula yakatya, “Magu ta, ane simanyile kono koyuli, hambi ane yo nili mudimi wo mwanakwetu?”
10 Nayo u Mulungu yakamutya Kayini, “Alu nghanici yiyi yono wuyinozize? Hambi isakami yo mwanakwenyu Habeli, nha yikundilila wulawa mu muhangha.
11 Alu agwe Kayini, hodu kulijitwa! Holeka sikosuma kujendelela kuwulima muhangha wuwuno wo moso, wono wahamuye u mulomo wayo, no kuying'wa i sakami yo mwanakwenyu Habeli, mono wumuwulaje gwegwe.
12 Na hono wijelele du wuwulime muhangha wuwo, hamba sikotujila lukulu cinhu co cose. Nagwe kanyhi kowa wusina wikalo ho hose, langa kowa kwanga caka du ako na ako mono'nze.”

13 Kayini nayo yakamupitula u Mulungu yakatya, “U wulipicizwe wangu nha wuwaha muno, mu nghango yono hamba sinosuma kuwudinghula.
14 Alu lanje ta, waminga ane i hanhu hono howuli muhangha wono wina moso, kanyhi sunga na ha mwande yako. Holeka nane ku wulawa lulu, nowa munhu mono nowa kwanga du ako na ako, na kanyhi nowa sina wikalo wono wuluwucile mono'nze, holeka u munhu wo wose du mono kombona, kosuma kumbulaga.”
15 Ninga u Mulungu yakamutya Kayini, “Ng'o, si nhavivyo! Yuyo mono kokuwulaga, ale kolipicizwa kali mupunghati.” Ale u Muwaha Mulungu yakawa kumuwicila Kayini citanjilo, u munhu wo wose mono komuwona ho yalece kumuwulaga.
16 Hodu nayo Kayini yakawa kuwuka ha mwande yo Mulungu, yakawa kubita kwikala mu yisi yono yili ku lubavu lwa kucilima ye Adeni, yono yikucemwa Nodi.

I zindelwa za Kayini

17 Kayini yakamumanya u mugolece wakwe, nayo mugolece wakwe yakatuga 'nda, yakasawa mwana mono yakucemwa Enoci. Kayini yakazenga likaya, yakalicemela litagwa lyo mwanagwe Enoci.
18 Nayo Enoci yakamulela Iladi, nayo Iladi yakamulela Mehuyayeli, Mehuyayeli nayo, yakamulela Metusayeli, nayo Metusayeli yakamulela Lameci,
19 nayo yuyo Lameci yakatola wacekulu wejete, yumonga yacemwaga Ada, no muyagwe Zila.
20 Ada yo yamulela Yabali, mono yali yo sogo ye wadima zimitugo, wono wekalaga mu mahema.
21 Ne litagwa lyo mwanakwawe, yali Yubali, mono yali yo sogo ye watowa masumbi ne zifulembe wanghula.
22 Zila nayo u mugolece we kejete, yakamulela Tubali-kayini, mono yali yo munghula wo kunoza viya vyose vye shaba na vye cuma. U lumbulye wa Tubali-kayini, yacemwaga Naama.

23 Lameci yakawalonjela i wagolece zakwe vyono wejete, wa Ada na Zila yakatya, “Ta muhulicizaje,

Muhulicizaje mugolile nye nyo wagolece zangu.

Ane no muyenyu, nimuwulaga u muzelelo yumonga, vyono mweneco

yo yamuye kunhowa no kumhela nghomele.

Ku soko yiyo holeka nane niwa kumuwulaga.”

24 “Holeka hono Kayini kolipicizwa kali mupunghati, ane yowa yinojeye nhuje wulipicizwe kali makumi mupunghati na mupunghati.”

Seti na Enoci

25 Adamu na Hawa, wakalela mwana yunji wa cilume. Nayo Hawa yakatya, “U Mulungu kamhela mwana yunji, kahaliziza ha Habeli mono yawulagwa na Kayini.”
26 Nayo Seti yakatuga mwana, yakamucemela litagwa lyakwe Enoci. Na baho ho wanhu wamulila kulicema litagwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu mu wuhembi wawo.


Mwamulizo 5

I zindelo za Adamu

(1Zin 1:1-4)

1 Awuno wo wityatye we zindelwa za Adamu. Mwi'pango lyono Mulungu yamuwumbila u munhu, yamuwumba mu futa yakwe mweneco.
2 Nayo yawawumba u mulume no mucekulu, hanyuma yakawa kuwalugutiza, yakawacemela ne litagwa lya “Munhu.”
3 Na mwi'pango lono Adamu yali yaficila myaka yigana limonga na makumi madatu, vilo vyo wikale wakwe, yakatuga mwana we hwani yakwe mweneco, nayo yakamucemela litagwa lya Seti.
4 Yali yeza kukondya kumulela Seti, u mwene Adamu yakekala myaka magana munana, nayo yakawa yatuga wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
5 Adamu yakafwa yena myaka magana yicenda na makumi madatu, yo wikale wakwe mono'nze.

6 Na lono Seti, yali yafisa myaka yigana limonga na mihano, yakamulela Enosi.
7 Nayo yaza kuwa yamulela Enosi, mwene Seti yakekala kanyhi myaka magana munana na mupunghati, nayo yakawa yena wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
8 Nayo Seti yakafwa yena myaka magana yicenda na yikumi na myejete.
9 Na lono Enosi, yali yaficila myaka makumi yicenda, baho yakamulela Kenani.
10 Na lono yali yamulela Kenani, mwene Enosi, yakekala kanyhi myaka magana munana na yikumi na mihano, nayo yakawa na wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
11 Nayo Enosi yakafwa yena myaka magana yicenda na mihano, yo wikale wakwe wa baha mono'nze.
12 Nayo Kenani, lono yali yaficila myaka makumi mupunghati, yakamulela Mahalaleli.
13 Nayo yali yeza kumulela Mahalaleli, mwene Kenani yakekala kanyhi myaka magana munana na makumi mane, nayo yakawa na wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
14 Nayo Kenani yakafwa yena myaka magana yicenda ne 'kumi limonga, yo wikale wakwe wa mono'nze.
15 Nayo Mahalaleli, lono yali yaficila myaka makumi mutandatu na mihano, yakamulela Yaledi.
16 Nayo yali yeza kumulela Yaledi, mwene yakekala kanyhi myaka magana munana na makumi madatu, nayo yakawa yena wana wanji walume na we cicekulu.
17 Mahalaleli yakafwa yena myaka magana munana na makumi yicenda na mihano, yo wikale wakwe wa mono'nze.

18 Nayo Yaledi, lono yali yaficila myaka yigana limonga na makumi mutandatu na myejete, yakamulela Enoci.
19 Nayo yali yeza kumulela Enoci, mwene Yaledi yakekala kanyhi myaka magana munana, nayo yali yena wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
20 Yaledi yakafwa yena myaka magana yicenda na makumi mutandatu na myejete, yo wikale wakwe wa mono'nze.
21 Nayo Enoci, lono yali yaficila myaka makumi mutandatu na mihano, yakamulela Metusela.
22 Nayo yali yeza kumulela Metusela, mwene Enoci, yakekala kanyhi myaka magana madatu, akuno vili hamonga no Mulungu. Nayo yakawa yena wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
23 Nayo Enoci yekala myaka magana madatu na makumi mutandatu na mihano yose hamonga.
24 Nayo vikajendana no Mulungu mu vilo vyakwe vyose, hanyuma yakahama hamba kwijela kanyhi. Hambi u Mulungu nha yamusola.

25 Nayo Metusela, lono yali yaficila myaka yigana limonga na makumi munana na mupunghati, nayo yakamulela Lameci.
26 Nayo yali yeza kumulela Lameci, mwene Metusela yakekala kanyhi myaka magana mupunghati na makumi munana na myejete, nayo yakawa na wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
27 Nayo Metusela yakafwa yena myaka magana yicenda na makumi mutandatu ne'cenda, yo wikale wakwe wa mono'nze.
28 Nayo Lameci, lono yali yeza kuficila myaka yigana limonga na makumi munana na myejete, nayo yakamulela mwana wa cilume.
29 No mwana yuyo, Lameci yakamucemela litagwa lya Noha, akuno yakutya, “U mwana yuyuno, yo kocisindamika mu milimo yetu mizito, yono cetuma kwe miwoko yetu, mono yisi yiyino yono u Mulungu yayilijita.”
30 Nayo Lameci yali yeza kumulela Noha, mwene Lameci yakekala kanyhi myaka magana mahano na makumi yicenda na mihano, nayo yakawa na wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
31 Nayo Lameci yakafwa yena myaka magana mupunghati na makumi mupunghati na mupunghati, yo wikale wakwe wa mono'nze.
32 Nayo Noha, lono yali yaficila myaka magana mahano yo wikale wakwe, yakawalela wa Semu na Hamu na Yafeti.


Mwamulizo 6

U wubi we wanhu

1 I wanhu wali weza kuwa wonjelezeka mono'nze, sunga ne wana we cicekulu wonjelezeka kulelwa kuwa wenji,
2 viwumbwa vya kucanyha, vikawawona wawo wana we cicekulu we wanhu vyono waswanu, navyo vikawatola wose wono viwendile.
3 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “U Muhe wangu si vyoswa vikulewana no munhu cibitilila, hambi mwene nha muwili du. Holeka i myaka yakwe yo wikale wakwe mono'nze, si yociza myaka yigana limonga na makumi mejete yaye.”
4 Mu vilo vivilya, sunga na hanyuma yayo, kwali kogali manhu mono'nze, gono galelwa ne viwumbwa vya kucanyha ku wana we cicekulu we wanhu. Wawo wo wali wanhu we vilungo muno, kanyhi wakawa ne lilumbo yiwaha muno mu vilo vivyo. Ne wanhu wawo wacemwaga “Wanefili.”

5 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu, yaza kuwuwona u wubi we wanhu mono'nze, na vyono i lihala lye mahweso ga mu zinhumbula zawo, likalaga yibi du nhondo zose,
6 u Mulungu yakakalala muno muno so kumuwumba u munhu mono'nze. U Muwaha Mulungu yakakalala muno muno mu nhumbula yakwe.
7 Holeka yakatya, “Hodu, nomuwusiza lukulu mono'nze, u munhu mono namuwumba, ne vilimu, ne vitambala hasi, hamonga ne zindeje za mu mbago. Nghukalala muno muno no kwihwesa vyono, ku sokoci nawawumba no kuwawika mono'nze.”
8 Ninga u Mulungu yakendezwa na Noha.

Kuli za Noha

9 Awuno wo wityatye we zinghani za Noha. Yuyo Noha yo yali yo munhu mono yali yali ling'ani du yali wene, mono yali yasina yilema mu wikale wakwe. Noha vyawezanaga hamonga no Mulungu.
10 Nayo Noha yali yena wanagwe wadatu, nha cinji yali Semu, Hamu na Yafeti.
11 U Mulungu yakayiwona yinze vyono yali yahodanjika muno muno, no kumema wugazi.
12 Heya, u Mulungu yakayilanga yinze, nayo yakayiwona vyono yawa yihodanjice, hambi i wanhu wose nha weza wali mu mikalile mibi.
13 Holeka u Mulungu yakamulonjela Noha yakatya, “Nakondya kulamula, niwamale wanhu wose, hambi nha wayimemya yinze yose no wugazi wawo. Heya, nowongomezeza lukulu, hamonga ne yinze yose!
14 Holeka agwe lu, winozezaje Safina ko mubici we nghulunje. Wuvigawanule vyumba mugati yayo, no kuyihiliwa lami mugati na kunze yayo.
15 Nagwe yiyo Safina, wuyinozaje nhavi, u wutali wayo, wuweze mita yigana limonga na makumi madatu ne'cenda, no wugazi wayo, wuweze mita makumi mejete na yihano, no wima wayo wuweze mita yikumi na yihano.
16 Safina yiyo, yiweze na golofa yidatu, zono zina no mulango ha lubavu lwayo. Wuyinozezaje ne litembe, hanyuma wulece cilejeho ca nusu mita nhavi hagati ye litembe ne dali yayo.
17 Nane noleta lihwazi yiwaha lye malenga, ho sume kuvyongomezeza lukulu i viwumbwa vyose vyumi vyono vili mono'nze. Ne viwumbwa vyose vyofwila lukulu.
18 Ninga nonoza cilagane hagati yangu na gwegwe. Agwe kokwinjila mugati mu Safina, agwe hamonga no mugolece wako, hamonga ne wanago wose ne wagolece zawo.
19 Nagwe kovinjiza mu Safina ne vilimu vye mawala gose, kovinjiza vyejete vyejete, cilume ne cicekulu, ho vikale vyumi hamonga na gwegwe.
20 Nagwe kozinjiza ne zindeje zono zikwanga mucanyha, ze mawala gose, hamonga ne vilimu vye mawala gose, ne mawala gose ge viwumbwa vyono vitambalaga hasi, vyose vivyo, kovinjiza vyejete vyejete, ho wusume kuvikamisa vyumi.
21 Kanyhi wusoleleze ne zindigwa ze mawala gose, wuzikamise so ndigwa yenyu, hamonga na ye viwumbwa vyose vyono wina navyo mumo mu safina.”
22 Nayo Noha yakazinoza zose, nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu yamulajiliza.


Mwamulizo 7

Lihwazi lye malenga

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela Noha yakatya, “Alu, vyono yo gwegwe du, mono nghuwene yo wuli munhu we ling'ani, mu vilo vyako vyose, ale, alu winjile mu Safina, gwegwe, hamonga ne mhuga yako yose.
2 Ninga wusoleleze hamonga ne vilimu vyono vyelile, mupunghati mupunghati, nume ne nyhekulu, na kuli avilya vyono si vyelile, wuvisolaje vyejete vyejete du, nume ne nyhekulu.
3 Kanyhi wusoleleze ne zindeje zono zikwanga mucanyha, nazo wuzisolaje mupunghati mupunghati, nyhekulu ne nume. Na yiyi ho yiwe yo nzila yo kusuma kuyiwicila i mbeyu yayo, ho yijendelele kuwa mono'nze.
4 Nane kolo vyapuluta vilo mupunghati, notonyeza mvula mbaha muno muno, mu vilo makumi mane misi ne cilo, hamba kuceheza yaye. Nane novyongomezeza lukulu i viwumbwa vyose vyumi vyono navinoza.”
5 Nayo Noha, yakazinoza zose nha vyono u Muwaha Mulungu yamulajiliza.

6 Nayo Noha yali yaficile myaka magana mutandatu, lono Mulungu yaletela lihwazi mono'nze.
7 Nayo Noha no mugolece wakwe, wanagwe nawo ne wagolece zawo, wose wakenjila mu Safina, ho wasume kupona mu wufwila mwi'hwazi lye malenga.
8 Ne vilimu vyose vyono vyelile, na vyose vyono si vyelile, ne zindeje hamonga ne viwumbwa vyose vyono vitambalaga hasi,
9 vyose vikenjila vyejete vyejete mugati mu Safina hamonga na Noha, nha vyono Mulungu yamulajiliza Noha.
10 Navyo vilo mupunghati vyali vyeza kupuluta, hodu lihwazi lye malenga likawa kuza mono'nze.

11 Nayo Noha lono yali yaficila myaka magana mutandatu, mwi'zuwa lye likumi na mupunghati lya mu mwezi we kejete wa mu mikalile yakwe, mwi'zuwa lilyo, i mivwilo ye malenga yono yili hasi muno muno ye'si, yikawa kwanza kuvwila no kuhuluma kwe vilungo muno muno. Madilisha ga ku wulanga nago gakadiguka.
12 Ne mvula yikawa kutonya mono'nze mu vilo makumi mane misi ne cilo, hamba kuceheza yaye.
13 Nayo Noha, hamonga no mugolece wakwe, ne wana wawo wa Semu na Hamu na Yafeti, hamonga ne wagolece zawo, wose wakenjila mu Safina mwi'zuwa lilyo lono mvula yamulila kutonya.
14 Nawo wakenjila weneco, hamonga ne mawala gose ge vilimu vya mu mbago, ne mawala gose ge zimitugo, ne mawala gose ge vilimu vyono vitambalaga, hamonga ne mawala gose ge zindeje.
15 Nayo Noha yakenjila mu Safina hamonga ne viwumbwa vyose vyono vikwehela, vyejete vyejete,
16 nha vyono Mulungu yamulajiliza. Nayo Mulungu yakawa kuwudinda u mulango we Safina.

17 Nalyo lihwazi likawa mono'nze vilo makumi mane. I malenga gali geza kucila kumema, gakawa kuyinula Safina hacanyha ye yisi.
18 Nago malenga gakacila kumema muno muno mono'nze yose, sunga ne Safina yikawa kwelela mucanyha yago.
19 Ne malenga gago gajendelela konjelezeka kwe vilungo muno muno, sunga ne matunda i matali muno muno ga mono'nze, yose gakagubicizwa ne malenga.
20 Ne malenga gago gasuma kugagubiciza matunda, mita mupunghati na nusu hasi yago.
21 Ne viwumbwa vyose vyumi vya mono'nze, vyose vikafwa, zindeje, zimitugo, ne vilimu vya mu mbago, hamonga ne matewa gose ge viwumbwa vyose vya mu yisi nghavu, sunga ne wanhu wose kono walila,
22 heya i viwumbwa vyose vyono vikwehela vya mu yisi nghavu, vyose vikafwa.
23 U Mulungu yakavyongomezeza lukulu i viwumbwa vyose vyumi vyono vyali vili mono'nze, wanhu wose, vilimu, ne viwumbwa vyono vitambalaga, sunga ne zindeje zose zono zangaga mucanyha, vivyo vyose, vikongomezezwa lukulu. Wono wasigala, wali wo wa Noha du, hamonga na wawalya wono wali wali hamonga na Noha mugati ye Safina.
24 Ne malenga gago gakekala mono'nze mu vilo yigana limonga na makumi mahano.


Mwamulizo 8

Wunyale we lihwazi lye malenga

1 U Mulungu yakamukumbucila Noha, hamonga ne vilimu vyose vyono vyali vili hamonga nayo mugati ye Safina. Mulungu yakaleta mbeho mbaha muno muno mono'nze, ne malenga gagalya gakawa kwanza kucepuka.
2 Ne mivwilo yose yono yalawaga hasi muno muno ye yisi, hamonga ne madilisha gono galetaga malenga ku wulawa ku wulanga, vyose vikadindwa. Sunga ne mvula nayo yikawa kuleka kutonya,
3 ne malenga nago gakawa kujendelela kucepuka mhola mhola mono'nze. Na vyali vyeza kupuluta vilo yigana limonga na makumi mahano, hodu malenga gakawa gakondya kucepuka muno muno.
4 Na mwi'zuwa lye likumi na mupunghati, lya mu mwezi wo mupunghati, safina yikawa kutima mucanyha ye litunda limonga lya mu matunda ga Alalati.
5 Ne malenga nago, gakajendelela kucepuka du mhola mhola, na mwi'zuwa lilongozi lya mu mwezi we likumi, i vizuli vye matunda vikawa kwanza kwijela.

6 Navyo vyali vyeza kupuluta vilo makumi mane, Noha yakawa kulidigula lidilisha lye Safina,
7 yakalilavyaniza lihowe kunze ye Safina, nalyo lihowe lilyo, hamba silyawuya kanyhi mu safina ng'o. Langa likajendelela kuguluka du ako na ako, sunga lono malenga ganyalila mono'nze.
8 Hanyuma, Noha yakayilavyaniza nziwa, ho yisume kuwona hono malenga gawe gakondya kucepuka.
9 Ninga vyono malenga gali gakali gayifunicize yinze yose, nziwa yiyo hamba siyasuma kuhatuga hanhu hono yosuma kutima yaye, holeka yikawa kumuwuyila kanyhi Noha mu Safina. Nayo Noha yakawugolola muwoko wakwe, yakayisola, no kuyiwika mugati ye Safina.
10 Nayo Noha, yakalindila kanyhi vilo mupunghati, yakayilavyaniza kanyhi nziwa yiyilya.
11 Nayo nziwa yiyo, yikamuwuyila kanyhi Noha mu Safina mu cipango ca saa ze cinyamihe, akuno yilumile ne linhambi lyo muzeyituni, mu mulomo wayo. Holeka Noha yakamanya vyono malenga gakondya kucepuka mono'nze.
12 Hanyuma yakalindila kanyhi vilo mupunghati vinji, yakayilavyaniza kanyhi nziwa yiyilya. No muhinzo wuwuno nziwa yiyo hamba siyamuwuyila kanyhi yaye.

13 Nayo Noha lono yali yaficila myaka magana mutandatu na wumonga, mwi'zuwa lilongozi lya mu mwezi mulongozi, malenga ge lihwazi, gakawa gakondya kunyalila lukulu mono'nze. Nayo Noha yakawa kucigubula cigubicizo ce Safina, nayo yali yeza kulanga kunze ye Safina, yakawona vyono yinze yakondya kunyala.
14 Na mwi'zuwa lye makumi mejete na mupunghati lya mu mwezi we kejete, yinze yali yakondya kunyalila lukulu.
15 Hobaho Mulungu yakawa kumulonjela Noha yakatya,
16 “Ale, wulawile mu Safina, gwegwe no mugolece wako, hamonga ne wanago wose ne wagolece zawo.
17 Wulavyanize vyose, zindeje zose, ne vilimu vyose, hamonga ne viwumbwa vyose vyono vitambalaga hasi, ho visume kwilela muno, no kwinjipa no kuhweguka kono'nze.”
18 Hodu, Noha yakawa kulawila, mweneco, no mugolece wakwe, ne wanagwe hamonga ne wagolece zawo,
19 ne vilimu vyose vya mu mbago, ne viwumbwa vyose vyono vitambalaga, hamonga ne zindeje zose, sunga ne vilimu vyose du i vyumi vya mono'nze, vyose vivyo, vikalawila mu Safina nha vyono vinha mu zimhuga zavyo.

Noha yakukumbika

20 Nayo Noha yakamuzenjela u Muwaha Mulungu cikumbicilo, yakawa kusola i vilimu vyose vyono vyelile vye mawala gose, ne zindeje, yakawa kuvilavya vilavywa vinyanywe mu wukumbicilo.
21 Ne mhunjile swanu ye vilavywa vivyo, yikamwendeza muno u Muwaha Mulungu, nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakatya mu nhumbula yakwe, “Hodu, hamba si nilayihodanjila lukulu yinze kanyhi, ku soko yo munhu, hambi nha nakondya kumanya vyono i mahweso go munhu, nha mabi du ku wulawa wudodo wakwe. Hamba si novyongomezeza lukulu kanyhi i viwumbwa vyose, nha vyono nozize.
22 Na mu mapango gose lono yinze yowa yikali, i madaha go kuhadika zimbeyu, no kududula, i madaha ge mbeho ne livuce, ne madaha ge cibahu ne cifuku, ne madaha ge misi ne cilo, hamba si vyoduma yaye.”


Mwamulizo 9

I cilagane co Mulungu na Noha

1 U Mulungu yakawalugutiza Noha ne wanagwe, yakawatya, “Ale, mwilele mwinjipe muno muno, no kuyimemya yinze.
2 Ne vilimu vyose, ne zindeje zose zono zikwanga mucanyha, ne viwumbwa vyose vyono vitambalaga hasi, hamonga ne zisomba zose, vivyo vyose vyowa vina woga vikumogopenyi nyenye. Hambi vyose vivyo nha mwapegwa, navyo vili mu miwoko yenyu.
3 Ne vilimu vyose i vyumi, avyo vyowa yo ndigwa yenyu, nha vyono namupelenyi vilimwa yiwe yo ndigwa yenyu.
4 Langa mulecele lukulu kulya nyama yono yina ne sakami yayo, vyono wumi wuli mumo mu sakami.
5 U munhu wo wose mono kowulaga munhu muyagwe, yuyo nayo kolipicizwa. Kanyhi nowulipiciza ko wuwulagwe, u mutugo wo wose wono wowulaga munhu.

6 “Hambi u munhu nha yawumbwa ku futa yo Mulungu.

Holeka munhu wo wose mono komuwulaga munhu,

u munhu yuyo nayo kowulagwa na munhu muyagwe.”

7 “Holeka anye mwilele mwinjipe,

mwilele ko winji sunga muyimemye yinze.”

8 Kanyhi Mulungu yakawalonjela Noha ne wanagwe yakatya,
9 “Aluno nghunoza cilagane cangu na nyenye hamonga no lulelo lwenyu lwa hanyuma yenyu,
10 kanyhi na kuli viwumbwa vyose vyumi, ne zindeje, ne zi mitugo, ne vilimu vyose vya mu mbago, vyose vyono vilawiye mu Safina, hamonga nanye.
11 Ane nghucikangaza i cilagane cangu na nyenye, nha vyono si nolokongomezeza lukulu kanyhi kwe lihwazi lye malenga i viwumbwa vyumi, hamba lihwazi lye malenga si lilejelela lukulu kanyhi ho lyongomeze yinze.
12 Na acino co citanjilo ce cilagane cono nghuciwika muli'ne na nyenye, hamonga ne viwumbwa vyumi vyose vyono vili hamonga na nyenye, sunga ku zindelo zono zikuza hanyuma.
13 Ane nghuwika licilalyambwa lyangu mwi'vunde, nalyo lyowa co citanjilo ce cilagane cangu ca kuli'ne ne yinze.
14 Na mu mapango go gose, lono noyigubiciziza yinze ne mavunde, nalyo licilalyambwa kolo likwijela mu mavunde,
15 ale baho nane nocikumbucila i cilagane cangu ca kuli'ne na nyenye, hamonga ne viwumbwa vyose vyumi.
16 Nalyo lilyo licilalyambwa, kolo lyejela du mu mavunde, nane noliwona, no kukumbucila kamonga i cilagane cico ce cibitilila cono cili kuli'ne ne viwumbwa vyose vyumi.
17 Na cico co citanjilo ce cilagane cono nakondya kuciwika kuli'ne ne viwumbwa vyose vyumi vyono vili mono'nze.”

Noha ne wanagwe

18 I wana wa Noha wono walawila mu Safina, wali wa Semu na Hamu na Yafeti. Hamu yakawa yo sogwe wa Kanani.
19 Na wawo wo wana wa Noha, wono ku wulawilila muli wene, i wanhu wakatandala mu yinze yose.
20 Nayo Noha, yali mulima migunda mono, nayo yali yo munhu mulongozi wo kulima no mugunda we mizabibu.
21 Na lono yali yaying'wa divayi no kugala, hodu yakawa kugona mwi'hema lyakwe yali cipwi.
22 Nayo Hamu u sogwe wa Kanani, yakaciwona i cipwi co sogwe, hanyuma yakawa kubita kunze, yakawa kuwalonjela wawalya i wanduguze wejete wa Semu na Yafeti.
23 Nawo wa Semu na Yafeti, wakawa kusola mwenda, wakawutandakula wakawa kuwutandaza mu mawega gawo. Wakawa kubita cinya mugongo, no kumufunika u sogo yawo. Hamba si wenda no kumulanga u sogo yawo, hamba ne cipwi co sogo yawo, si waciwona yaye.
24 Nayo u sogo yawo yali yeza kwenzeluka mu wugale wakwe, no kumanya vyono yamutendeye u mwanagwe mudodo,
25 hobaho yakawa kutya,

“Kanani yalijitwe!

Nayo kowa muwanda kuli wanduguze.”

26 Kanyhi yakatya,

“Nayo Semu, u Mulungu wangu yamulugutize

Nayo Kanani yaweze muwanda wa Semu.

27 U Mulungu yamonjeleze Yafeti

Nayo yekale mu mahema ga Semu.

Nayo Kanani yaweze muwanda wa Yafeti.”

28 Ne lihwazi lye malenga lyali lyeza kupuluta, Noha yakekala myaka magana madatu na makumi mahano,
29 Nayo yakafwa yena myaka magana yicenda na makumi mahano, ga mu wikale wakwe wa mono'nze.


Mwamulizo 10

I zindelo ze wana wa Noha

(1Zin 1:5-23)

1 I wana wa Noha, Semu na Hamu na Yafeti, lono lihwazi lye malenga lyali lyapuluta, nawo walela wana awano,
2 I wana wa Yafeti wali wo awa, Gomeli, Magogu, Madayi, Yavani, Tubali, Meshaki na Tilasi.
3 Nawo i wana wa Gomeli wali, Ashikenazi, Lifati, na Togalima.
4 Ne wana wa Yavani wali, Elisha, Talishishi, Kitimu na Dodanimu.
5 Awo wawo, wo mwamulizo we wanhu wono wakatandala ku zimbavu ze zi mhwani na mu vizingwa. Wawo zo ndelo za Yafeti wono wetemanuka temanuka, wakawa wanhu na yisi yawo, ne nonga yawo, mu nghango ye zimhuga zawo, mu zinghungugo zawo.

6 Nawo i wana wa Hamu wali, Kushi, Misili, Puti na Kanani.
7 Nawo wana wa Kushi, wali, Sheba, Havila, Sabuta, Lama na Sabuteka. Ne wana wa Lama wali, Sheba na Dedani.
8 Nayo yuyo Kushi, yali yo sogwe wa Nimulodi, mono yali yo citung'ati mulongozi muwaha mono'nze.
9 Na so lihewa lyo Mulungu, mwene Nimulodi, yakawa mupelembi muwaha. Na holeka kowuli mulongo wono wutezaga, “Nha vyono yenha Nimulodi u mupelembi muwaha ko wutazi wo Mulungu.”
10 U mwamulizo wo wutemi wakwe Nimulodi, wali Babiloni na Eleki na Akadi, mu yisi ya Shinali.
11 Na ku wulawilila kuko, yakawa kubita ku Ashuli, yakazenga makaya ga Ninawi, na Lehoboti-li, na Kala na
12 Leseni, wono wuli haciligati ye Ninawi ne likaya liwaha lya Kala.

13 Misili yali, yo kuku yawo i Waludi, Wanamu, Walehabi, Wanafutu,
14 Wapatulusi, Wakasilu (wono wo mwamulizo we Wafilisiti,) na Wakelete.
15 Nayo Kanani yali, yo kukuye wa Sidoni, u mwanagwe u mulongozi kulelwa, na Heti,
16 kanyhi ne Wayebusi, Waamoli, Wagiligashi,
17 Wahivi, Waliki, Wasini,
18 Walwadi, Wasemali, ne Wahamati. Na hanyuma yayo i wanhu we zimhuga hayo hayo za Kanani wakeyagala,
19 no mupaka we yisi yawo, wali wulawiliye Sidoni, sunga kutakama ku Gelali behi na Gaza, na ku wubita kucilima, sunga ku Sodoma na Gomola, na Adima na Seboyimu behi na Lasa.
20 Nawo wawo wo we ndelo ya Hamu, mu nghango ye zimhuga zawo, ne zinonga zawo, ne zi'si zawo, ne zinghungugo zawo.

21 Nayo Semu u muwahawe wa Yafeti, yali yo sogo we Wahebelaniya wose.
22 Ne wana wa Semu wali, Elamu, Ashuli, Alipakishadi, Ludi na Alamu.
23 Ne wana wa Alamu, wali Usi, Uli, Geteli, na Mashi.
24 Nayo Alipakishadi yakamulela Shela, nayo Shela yakamulela Ebeli.
25 Nayo Ebeli yakawa na wana wejete, u mwana mulongozi, yakamucemela litagwa lya Pelegu, ku soko vyono i wanhu mu vilo vivyo, wakawa wagawanuka mono'nze. Nayo we kejete yakamucemela litagwa lya Yokitani,
26 nayo Yokitani yakawalela, Alimodadi, Shelefu, Haasa Halimaweti, Yela,
27 Hadolamu, Uzali, Dikila,
28 Obali, Abimayeli, Sheba,
29 Ofili, Havila, na Yobabu. Wawo wose wali wana wa Yokitani.
30 Ne yisi yono wekalaga, yali yilawiliye ku wulawa Mesha, sunga ko ku Sefali mu matunda ga ku lubavu lwa kucilima.
31 Nawo wawo zo ndelo za Shemu, mu nghango ye zimhuga zawo, ne zinonga zawo, ne zisi zawo, ne zinghungugo zawo.
32 Na wawa wose, wo we mhuga ya Noha, mu nghango ye zindelo zawo mu zinghungugo zawo. No wulawilila muli wawo, i zinghungugo zose zikawa kwiyagala ako na ako mono'nze yose lono lihwazi lye malenga lyali lyapuluta.


Mwamulizo 11

Umunala wa Babiloni

1 Ku wulawa ku mwamulizo, i yinze yose yali yina milonjele mimonga du, ne zinghani zose zihwanile.
2 Alu i wanhu lono wali weza kwanza kujendajenda wakulawilila ku lubavu lwa kucilima, nawo wali weza kufika mu yisi yo wuhihi ya Shinali, hodu wakawa kwikala kuko.
3 Ninga hanyuma yayo, wakanza kwilonjela wakutya, “Nye, alenyi cinoze matofali, ciganyanye viswanu mu moto.” Holeka wakawa watuga matofali ne lami yo kugalunziza i matofali hamonga.
4 Nawo wakeyitya, “Alenyi le ciyinzenjele co weneco likaya lyono lyowa lina no munala wono wofika ku wulanga, ho cisume kwitujila litagwa, kanyhi ho cilece kwiyagala mono'nze yose.”

5 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu, yakawa kutima ho yaliwone likaya lilyo lyono wanhu walizenjile, hamonga no munala walyo.
6 U Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Alu, lanje ta, wanhu awa, wose wali nghungugo monga, kanyhi wose wakulonga milonjele mimonga, na awuno wo mwamulizo du wa zose zono wosaka kwituma. Na vyono yikwijela, ku wulawa lulu, wosuma kwituma yoyose yono wosaka kwituma.
7 Holeka alenyi le citime ciwabitile cikawewajize nonga yawo, ho walece kwihulika weneco wose.”
8 Holeka u Muwaha Mulungu yakawa kuweyagaziza mono yinze yose, wakawa kuwuleka no mulimo wu wulya wo wuzenga likaya lilyo.
9 Ne likaya lilyo likacemwa likaya lya Babiloni, vyono ho baho hono u Mulungu yawewajiziza i wanhu wose milonjele yawo. Na kanyhi ha wulawilila baho, ho Mulungu yaweyagaziza i wanhu wakeyagala mono yinze yose.

I zindelo za Semu

(1Zin 1:24-27)

10 Awano wo we ndelo ya Semu. Yali yeza kupuluta myaka myejete ku wulawa he 'hwazi lye malenga, nayo Semu lono yali yeza yaficila myaka yigana limonga, yakamulela Alipakishadi.
11 Nayo lono yali yamulela Alipakishadi, mwene Semu yekala kanyhi myaka magana mahano, nayo yakawa na wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
12 Nayo Alipakishadi, yamulela Shela lono yali yaficila myaka makumi madatu na mihano.
13 Alipakishadi lono yali yakondya kumulela Shela, u mwene Alipakishadi yakekala kanyhi myaka magana mane na midatu, nayo yakawa na wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.

14 Nayo Shela, lono yamulelela Ebeli, yali yaficile myaka makumi madatu.
15 Nayo Shela lono yali yamulela Ebeli, mwene Shela yakekala kanyhi myaka magana mane na midatu, nayo yakawa na wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
16 Na Ebeli nayo, lono yali yaficila myaka makumi madatu na mine, yakamulela Pelegu.
17 Na lono yali yamulela Pelegu, u mwene Ebeli yekala kanyhi myaka magana mane na makumi madatu, nayo yakawa na wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
18 Lono Pelegu yali yaficila myaka makumi madatu, nayo yakamulela Lewu.
19 Nayo Pelegu lono yali yamulela Lewu, u mwene Pelegu yekala kanyhi myaka magana mejete ne 'cenda, nayo nhavivyo, yakawa yena wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.

20 Lewu nayo, lono yali yaficila myaka makumi madatu na myejete, yakamulela Selugi.
21 Nayo Lewu lono yali yamulela Selugi, u mwene Lewu yekala kanyhi myaka magana mejete na mupunghati, nayo yakawa yena wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.

22 Lono Selugi nayo, yali yaficila mu myaka makumi madatu, yakamulela Naholi.
23 U mwene Selugi yekala kanyhi myaka magana mejete lono yali yamulela Naholi, nayo yakawa na wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
24 Nayo Naholi lono yali yaficila mu myaka makumi mejete ne 'cenda, yamulela Tela.
25 Nayo Naholi yakekala kanyhi myaka yigana limonga ne kumi ne 'cenda lono yali yamulela Tela. Nayo yakawa na wana wanji we cilume na we cicekulu.
26 Nayo Tela lono yali yaficila myaka makumi mupunghati, yakawalela Abulamu, Naholi na Halani.

I zindelo za Tela

27 Azi ahano zo ndelo ze wana wa Tela, u sogo ya wa Abulamu, Naholi na Halani. Yuyu Halani yali yo sogo wa Loti.
28 U mwene Halani yafwila ku Uli, likaya lye Wakalidayo kono yalelelwa u sogwe, lono sogwe Tela yali yakali mumi.
29 Nawo wa Abulamu na Naholi, wose wakawa kutola. U mugolece wa Abulamu yacemwaga Salayi. No mugolece wa Naholi, nayo yacemwaga Milika u mwana wa Halani, mono kanyhi yali yo sogo wa Isika.
30 Nayo Salayi yakawa yasina mwana, hambi nha yali si kalelaga.
31 Nayo Tela yakawa kuwasola u mwanagwe Abulamu, hamonga na Loti u mwizukuluwe, mono yali yo mwana wo mwanagwe Halani, hamonga na Salayi u mugolece wa Abulamu. Wakawa kutuluka wose hamonga ku wulawa ku Uli, likaya lye Wakalidayo, wakubita ku yisi ye Wakanani. Ninga wali weza kufika mu Halani, hodu wakekala kuko.
32 Nayo Tela yakafwila kuko ku Halani, yali yaficile myaka magana mejete na mihano.


Mwamulizo 12

U lucemo lo Mulungu kuli Abulamu

1 Ale, u Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela Abulamu yakatya, “Ale wuwuce mu yisi yako, wuyilece ne mhuga yako wose sunga ne kaya yo sogo, wubite sunga ku yisi yono nene nokulajila.”
2 “Nane nokunoza wuwe yikungugo yiwaha muno muno, kanyhi nokulugutiza, nane nolinoza litagwa lyako limanyice muno, nagwe wuwe wumoti.”

3 “Nayo mono kokulugutiza, nane nomulugutiza.

Nayo mono yakukulijita, nane nomulijita.

Ne zimhuga zose za kono'nze zolomotela muli gwegwe.”

4 Hodu du, Abulamu yakawa kutuluka nha vyono Mulungu yamulajiliza, vikawa kubitana hamonga na Loti. Nayo Abulamu yali yaficile myaka makumi mupunghati na mihano lono yawucila ku Halani.
5 Yakamusoleleza no mugolece wakwe Salayi, hamonga na Loti u mwana wo mwanakwawe Halani, hamonga ne sawo yawo yose yono wali wayisawa, wakawa wasoleleza hamonga ne wanhu wose wono wawatuga kuko ku Halani. Nawo wakatuluka wakubita ku Kanani.

Nawo wali weza kufika mu yisi ya Kanani.
6 Mwene Abulamu yakakolela haciligati ye yisi, yakawa kubita sunga ko ku Shekemu, i hanhu hono helile, hanhu hono ho wuli no mubici wo munghola wa Mole. Na mu vilo vivyo, Wakanani wo wali wekaye mu yisi yiyo.
7 Ale, u Muwaha Mulungu yakamulawila Abulamu yakamutya, “Nolupela lulelo lwako yisi yiyino.” Co caleka Abulamu yakumuzenjela cikumbicilo u Muwaha Mulungu mono yamulawila.
8 Hanyuma Abulamu yakawa kutuluka, yakawa kubita mu matunda ga ku lubavu lwa kucilima ye Beteli. Nayo yakawa kulikoma lihema lyakwe kuko, haciligati. Likaya lye Beteli yakulilanga ku lubavu lwe mwezi, likaya lya Ayi nalyo, yakulilanga ku lubavu lwe cilima. Naho baho, Abulamu yakamuzenjela u Muwaha Mulungu cikumbicilo, yakamuhemba.
9 Hanyuma Abulamu, yakajendelela no muhinzo wakwe du yalanjile ku lubavu lwa Negebu.

Abulamu ku Misili

10 Na mu vilo vivyo, yikagwa nzala mbaha mu yisi ya Kanani. Ne nzala yiyo yikawa mbaha muno muno. Holeka yikawa yimunojeye Abulamu yabite ku Misili, yakekale kuko nha muhamizi.
11 Nayo Abulamu lono yali yeza yali behi no kwinjila mu Misili, yakawa kumulonjela u mugolece wakwe Salayi yakutya, “Gwe mwendece, ane manyile vyono, agwe wuli mucekulu mono wina futa swanu muno muno ku wulangwe.
12 Holeka Wamisili kolo wakukuwona, wotya, ‘Ayu mugolece wakwe.’ Hanyuma lu'ne wombulaga, ninga agwe si wokuwulaga yaye.
13 Holeka nghukunena, agwe wuwatezaje vyono wuli lumbu lyangu, holeka ne zinghani zowa swanu kuli'ne, nane ho nosuma kuwa mumi ku soko yako.”
14 Nayo Abulamu yali yeza kufika mu yisi ya Misili, wanya wusi wa kuko, wakamuwona Salayi vyono yo mucekulu muswanu muno muno.
15 Nawo wawaha wa mu wutemi wa Falawo, wali weza kumuwona Salayi, hobaho wakawa kubita kumukumizya u mutemi Falawo zo wuswanu muno wa Salayi. Hodu, Salayi yakawa kutegulwa ku kaya ya Falawo.
16 Ale, ku soko ya Salayi, i zinghani zikawa kumuwela swanu Abulamu. Nayo Abulamu yali yena hamonga ne zimitugo zakwe, zingholo, zing'ombe, hamonga ne zindogowe zakwe ne zingamiya zakwe, hamonga ne wetumami wakwe i walume na we cicekulu.

17 Ninga u Muwaha Mulungu, yakamugumilila Falawo wugazwe wuwaha muno muno we matamwa, u mwene hamonga ne wanhu wa mu nyumba yakwe wose, ku soko ya Salayi u mugolece wa Abulamu.
18 Holeka Falawo yakamucema Abulamu yakamuwuza yakatya, “Alo nghanici yiyi waco yono wunhendeye? Alu nhawule si wamanyiza vyono yuyu Salayi yo mugolece wako?
19 Na socici wukalonga vyono Salayi lumbulyo, nagwe wukawa kunyambuza, nane nghawa kumusola, yawe mugolece wangu? Ale lu mugolece wako ayu aha. Holeka lu, wumusolaje mubitane nayo!”
20 Hodu Falawo yakawa kuwalajiliza wanhu wakwe, wakawa kumusindiciza Abulamu, no mugolece wakwe, hamonga ne zisawo zakwe zose zono yali yena nazo.


Mwamulizo 13

U witemanuce wa Abulamu na Loti

1 Abulamu yakawuya ku wulawa ku Misili, yakabita ku Negebu. Nayo yali yali hamonga no mugolece wakwe ne sawo yakwe yose, hamonga na Loti.
2 Na mu vilo vivyo, Abulamu yali yagolipa muno. Yali yena mitugo, ne mapesa hamonga ne zizahabu.
3 Nayo Abulamu yakajendelela no muhinzo wakwe ku wulawa ku Negebu sunga yakafika ku Beteli. Yakafika hanhu hono yali yalikomile lihema lyakwe halulongozi, haciligati ya Beteli na Ayi,
4 hanhu hono yazenga i cikumbicilo. Nayo yakamuhemba u Muwaha Mulungu.
5 Na Loti mono vyetyatya hamonga na Abulamu, nayo nhavivyo, yali yena zingholo ne zimitugo hamonga ne mahema.
6 Ne liwalo lye zimitugo zawo, lyali yiwaha muno muno, sunga ne yisi yikawa si yikuwakwila ku wikala hamonga wose Abulamu na Loti.
7 Ale baho, wukawa kwijela wugombane hagati ye wadimi wa Abulamu ne wadimi wa Loti. Na mu vilo vivyo, Wakanani ne Wapelizi, wo wali wekazi wa mu yisi yiyo.

8 Holeka Abulamu yakamulonjela Loti yakatya, “Gwe mwanangu, si viswanu hawe na mazozo no wugombane hagati yetu, hamba hagati ye wadimi wako ne wadimi wangu, hambi ase nha cili ndugu.
9 Alu wulanje ta, yisi yiyi yose yili ha mwande yako. Holeka yowa yili ciba, hono citemanuce. Hono agwe wusace kubita ku lubavu lo kucekulu, ane nane nobita ku lubavu lo kulume, na hono wuhagule kubita ku lubavu lo kulume, ane nobita ku lubavu lo kucekulu.”
10 Nayo Loti yakenula i meso gakwe, yakayilanga nyika yose ya Yoludani, nayo yakayiwona vyono yina malenga menji hanhu hose gono gakwiliye. Nayo yinha kotya lilolo lyo Mulungu, na kotya yisi ya Misili ku lubavu lwa Sowali, lono Mulungu yali yakali nagongomeza imakaya ga Sodoma na Gomola.
11 Holeka Loti yakawa kubita ku lubavu lwe cilima, nayo yakahagula lilolo lyose lya Yoludani, nawo ku wulawa lulo baho, wakawa kwitemanuka.
12 Ninga mwene Abulamu yakasigala mu yisi ya Kanani. Mwene Loti yakekala haciligati ye makaya gono gali gali mwi'lolo, nayo yakahamizya lihema lyakwe ku Sodoma.
13 Ale, i wanhu wa kuko ku Sodoma, wali wanhu wabi muno, kanyhi wali wahodanji wawaha hali Mulungu.

Abulamu yakuhamila ku Hebuloni

14 Nayo Loti lono vyali vyakondya kwitemanuka na Abulamu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela Abulamu yakatya, “Ale ta, winule meso gako ku wulawilila baho hono wimile, nagwe wulanje zimbavu zose, kusumuka, kutakama, kucilima na kumwezi.
15 Yisi yiyino yose yono wukuyiwona, ane nokupela gwegwe hamonga no lulelo lwako, yiwe yisi yenyu ko ku cibitilila.
16 No lulelo lwako nowanoza wawe wenji muno muno kotya muhangha we yinze. Nha vyono muhangha si wosuma kuwalwa, ale, nhavivyo no lulelo lwako nalo, si losumika ku wuwalwe!
17 Holeka alu winuce wuyipilime pilime yisi yiyi yose, wuyilanje mu wima wayo no wugazi wayo, hambi nha nokupela gwegwe.”
18 Hodu Abulamu yakawa kulikula lihema lyakwe, yakawa kubita kwikala behi ne miyaloni, i mibici ye minghola ya Mamule, yono yili mu Hebuloni, nako kuko, yakamuzenjela Muwaha Mulungu cikumbicilo.


Mwamulizo 14

Abulamu yakumuponya Loti

1 Na mu vilo vivyo, u mutemi Amulafeli wa ku Shinali, no mutemi Aliyoki wa Elasali, no mutemi Kedolilayoma wa Elamu, no mutemi Tidali wa Goyimu,
2 watemi wawo wose wakawa kwigaluka, wakawa kwihoma na Bela u mutemi wa Sodoma, Bilisha u mutemi wa Gomola, Shinabu u mutemi wa Adima, Shemebeli u mutemi wa Zeboyimu, no mutemi wa Bela (Yo Sowali.)
3 Ne watemi wawo wose wahano, wakawa kuwakongholela hamonga i wasilikale wawo wo wulugu mwi'lolo lya Sidimu, (wo wutya, i Lilenga lyo Mwino.)
4 Nawo wakajendelela kumuhulika u mutemi Kedolilayoma mu myaka yikumi na myejete, ninga wali weza kufika mwaka we likumi na midatu, hodu wakawa kumubeza.
5 Na mu mwaka we likumi na yine, u mutemi Kedolilayoma hamonga na wawalya watemi wayagwe, wakaza wakawahoma no kuwasuma i wanhu wa ku Lefayimu kuko ku Ashiteloti-kalinayimu, hamonga ne Wazuzimi wa ku Hamu, ne Wayemi wa ku Shawe-kuliyatayimu,
6 ne Waholi mu matunda gawo ku Seyili. Wakawawinga wakawafisa sunga ko ku Elipalani mu mipaka ya mu nyika.
7 Hanyuma wakawa kuwuya ne nyuma sunga ko (ku Enimishipati) ku Kadeshi. Nawo wakayihoma yisi yose ye Waamaleki, no kuwasumiza Waamoli wono wekalaga mu Hazazonitamali.

8 U mutemi wa Sodoma, no mutemi wa Gomola, no mutemi wa Adima, no mutemi wa Zeboyimu, no mutemi wa Bela,
9 (wo wutya Sowali) nawo wakenjila mu wulugu, mwi'lolo lya Sidimu, wakawa kwihoma na Kedolilayoma, na Amulafeli u mutemi wa Shinali, na Aliyoki u mutemi wa Elasali. Watemi wawo wane, wakawa kwihoma ne watemi wahano.
10 Nalyo lilolo lilyo lya Sidimu, lyali lina makombokombo ga lami, ne watemi wawo wa Sodoma na Gomola lono wamanghaga wakutiza wulugu, wakawa kuzubujila mugati ye makombo gago. Ninga awalya iwadatu wanji, awo watoloka no kubita mu matunda.
11 Hodu, awalya wono wawasuma wayawo, wakawa kuzisola zisawo zose za mu Sodoma na Gomola, hamonga ne vilimwa vyose, wakabita navyo.
12 Wakamusoleleza na Loti nayo, u mwana wa Halani u mwanakwawe wa Abulamu, mono yekalaga kuko ku Sodoma, hamonga ne zisawo zakwe zose, wakawa kubita nayo.

13 Munhu yunji mono yatoloka kuko ku wulugu, yakabita yakamulonjela Abulamu, u Muhebelaniya, mono yekalaga behi ne minghola, i mibici yono yelile yono yali ya Mamule, yuyulya Mwamoli. Nayo Mamule ne wanduguze wa Eshikoli na Aneli wali wakondya kwilumbaniza na Abulamu.
14 Nayo Abulamu yali yeza kulonjelwa vyono mwanagwe Loti yasolwa mateka, hodu ho baho baho yakawa kuwasola wanhu vitung'ati magana madatu na yikumi na munana, wono waleleywe mu nyumba yakwe, yakawa kuwatyatya wawalya walugu sunga ko ku Dani.
15 Nayo yali yeza kufika kuko, yakawa kuwagawanula wanhu wakwe mu vipuga vipuga. Na yali yeza kuwa mu cilo mugati, yakawahoma walugu wakwe, yakawasumiza, yakawawinjiliza sunga ko ku Hoba, kusumuka ya Damesiki.
16 Abulamu yakawa kuyikomboza sawo yose yono yali yasolwa ne walugu, yakamukomboza no mwanagwe Loti mweneco, hamonga ne sawo yakwe, hamonga ne wacekulu ne wanhu wanji.

U Mukohani Melikizedeki yakumulugutiza Abulamu

17 Nayo Abulamu lono yawuyaga yakulawa ku wulugu, lono yali yawasuma wa Kedolilayoma hamonga ne wayagwe, u mutemi wa ku Sodoma, yakawa kubita kumuhinjiliza Abulamu, mwi'lolo lya Shawe (wo wutya, Lilolo lyo Mutemi.)
18 Nayo Melikizedeki u mutemi we likaya lya Salemu, mono yali yo mukohani wo Mulungu Mono Yalawiliciye, yuyo yakaleteleza no mukate ne divayi kuli Abulamu,
19 yakamulugutiza Abulamu yakutya,

“U Muwaha Mulungu,

mono yawunoza wulanga ne yinze,

yamulugutize Abulamu!

20 U lukumyo lweze kuli Muwaha Mulungu

mono yakupelile vilungo vyo kuwasumila walugu wako!”

Nayo Abulamu yakawa kumulavizya Melikizedeki, lipinga lye monga kwe 'kumi lya muli vyose vyono yali yena navyo.
21 Nayo u mutemi wa Sodoma, yakamulonjela Abulamu yakatya, “Nghukunena, wunghombolelaje du wanhu, ninga zisawo zose wuzisolaje go mweneco.”
22 Nayo Abulamu yakamupitula yuyo mutemi wa Sodoma yakamutya, “Ane no muyago nghondya kwilahiza hali Muwaha Mulungu mono yawumba u wulanga ne yinze,
23 vyono sinosolela lukulu hamba luputi hamba musipi we cilatu, hamba cinhu cinji cako co cose, ho wulece kuza kuwona nha kotya ane ngolipa ku soko ye zisawo zako.
24 Holeka sinosuma kusola cinhu co cose, lunji ivinhu vyono waliye wanhu wangu, vivyo du langa vyo novibocela. Alu langa wuwakombolaje wa Aneli, na Eshikoli na Mamule, wo wasole ligawo lyawo.”


Mwamulizo 15

I cilagane co Mulungu kuli Abulamu

1 Hanyuma ya zizi, u Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjelela Abulamu mu wulawilwe yakatya, “Gwe Abulamu, wulece kogopa, ane nili ngula yako. Ne cipegwa cako cowa ciwaha kuciza!”
2 Ninga mwene Abulamu yakatya, “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, alu ta komhela cicici, nane kuno ho nghali sina 'mba mwana, no muhazi wangu yo yuyu du Eleyezali wa ku Damesiki?
3 Lanje ta, wukali hamba sinamhela mwana, no muwanda mono yaleleywe mu nyumba yangu, yuyu yo kowa yo muhazi wangu.”

4 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakamupitula Abulamu yakatya, “Ayu Eliyeza yo si kowa muhazi wako ng'o! Langa u mwana wa mu'nda yako gwegwe go mweneco, yuyo yo kowa muhazi wako.”
5 Baho u Muwaha Mulungu yakawa kumulavyaniza Abulamu hanze ye lihema, yakawa kumutya, “Abulamu, wugalamile ta, wulanje ku wulanga! Ale ta, wuziwalaje zinyelezi hono kosuma kuziwala! Ale no lulelo lwako vyo lowa lwinji nhavivyo!”
6 Nayo Abulamu yakawa kumwihuwila Mulungu, nayo Mulungu yakamuvumila Abulamu nha vyono yali ling'ani.
7 U Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela Abulamu, yakatya, “Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu mono nakuleta bahano ku wulawa ku Uli, likaya lye Wakalidayo, ho nghupele yisi yiyi yiwe yako.”
8 Ninga Abulamu yakapitula yakamutya, “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, alu ane nomanya nhawule vyono noyihala yisi yiyi?”
9 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakamutya Abulamu, “Ale Abulamu, wundetelaje ta ndama ye myaka midatu, ne mhene nyhekulu ye myaka midatu, ne ngholo nduwilu ye myaka midatu, hamonga ne nziwa ne nyana yayo.”
10 Nayo Abulamu yakamuletela u Muwaha Mulungu vinhu vivyo vyose, nayo Abulamu, yakawa kuvisanhanga vyose vikalawa hejete hejete, yakawa kuvipanga micilili myejete, akuno viyidomile vyejete vyejete. Langa zi nziwa, zo siyazisanha zilawe hejete yaye.
11 Nazo zi'ndi lono zazaga ho zilye zinyama zizo, umwene Abulamu nayo yakawa yakuziwinga.

12 Ne lizuwa lyaza kuswa, Abulamu yakebatwa na nhongo nyinji muno muno, woga hamonga ne citi ciwaha, vikamuziza.
13 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela Abulamu yakatya, “Nghusaka wumanyile lukulu, vyono u lulelo lwako, lokwikala mu wujenzi, mu yisi yono si yawo. Na kuko wowa wawanda, wolowetumamila myaka magana mane.
14 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, nane nololiling'aniza lilyo likungugo lyono wololitumamila. Ninga hanyuma yayo wolowuka kuko akuno wena ne zisawo nyinji.
15 Ninga agwe go mweneco kokwikala myaka minji, sunga wuze wufwe wupolile no kuzikwa.
16 No lulelo lwako lwe kane, lo lolowuya baha kanyhi, vyono u wubi we Waamoli wukali namemelecela.”

17 Kukawa lizuwa lyaza kuwa lyagwila, ne citi cenjila, lanje, kwa nhinicizo du, likejela likombo lyono lyafunghaga lyosi, hamonga ne lizinga lyono lyakaga moto, vikakolela hagati ye zisanho zizilya ze zinyama.
18 Na mwi'zuwa lilyo, ho Muwaha Mulungu yacinozeza i cilagane cakwe na Abulamu, yakumutya, “Gwe Abulamu, ane nowapela u lulelo lwako yisi yiyi, ku wulawilila kwi ‘kolongo lya Nile lya Misili, sunga ko kwi’ kolongo lililya liwaha lya Fulata.
19 Wo wutya i zisi ze Wakeni, Wakenizi, Wakadimoni,
20 Wahiti, Wapelizi, Walefayi,
21 Waamoli, Wakanani, Wagiligashi ne Wayebusi.”


Mwamulizo 16

Kuli za Hagali na Ishumayeli

1 Salayi u mugolece wa Abulamu, yali yakali namulelela Abulamu mwana. Ninga lulo, mwene Salayi, yali yena mwitumami we cicekulu munya Misili, ne litagwa lyakwe yacemwaga Hagali.
2 Ale, Salayi yakamulonjela mulume wakwe Abulamu yakatya, “Gwe mulume wangu, wumanyile vyono Mulungu yakali namhela wana. Holeka nghukunena, winjile kuli mwitumami wangu we cicekulu Hagali, na lunji yalandelela nane mwana.” Nayo Abulamu yakawuvumila u wulonje we mhuga yakwe Salayi.

3 Hodu Salayi yakawa kumukombola Abulamu yenjile kuli u mwitumami wakwe Hagali mu Misili, yawe mugolece wakwe. Nazo zizino zejela lono Abulamu yali yekala mu yisi ye Wakanani myaka yikumi.
4 Nayo Abulamu yakawa kumusola Hagali, nayo Hagali yakawa kudinghula 'nda. Ninga lulo baho Hagali yali yeza kuwona vyono yakondya kudinghula 'nda, hodu yakawa kwanza kumubedegala Salayi.
5 Ale, Salayi yakamutya Abulamu, “U wubi wono nghutendelwa na Hagali, ale, weze wako gwegwe. Hambi ane nha nakukombolela u mwitumami wangu Hagali, ninga alu, yali yeza kuwona vyono yatuga 'nda, hodu du, ane neza si nili cinji kanyhi ha meso gakwe! Ale du, u Muwaha Mulungu, yo koling'aniza hagati yetu na gwegwe!”
6 Ninga nayo Abulamu yakamutya Salayi, “Gwe mugolece wangu, ayu mwitumami wako, kanyhi yali mu miwoko yako. Holeka wudahile kumunozeza nha vyono wukusaka gwegwe du go mweneco.” Hodu Salayi yakawa kwanza kumukobyanza Hagali, sunga nhondo Hagali yakawa kutoloka.

7 Malayika wo Muwaha Mulungu yakamwagana Hagali mu nyika, hono howuli muvwilo we malenga, u muvwilo wono wuli mu nzila yono yibitile ku Shuli.
8 Malayika yakamuwuza Hagali yakutya, “Gwe Hagali go mwitumami wa Salayi, alu wukulawilila hayi, na kanyhi wukubita hayi?” Nayo Hagali yakatya, “Ane nghumutiza yaya Salayi.”
9 Nayo Malayika wo Muwaha Mulungu yakamutya Hagali, “Ale wumuwuyilaje yuyo nyina yako Salayi, ninga kolo wawuya, wumwitumamilaje ko winenenyhezi.”
10 Hamonga na zizo kanyhi, Malayika wo Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela Hagali yakatya, “Nane nolwinjifya muno muno u lulelo lwako, sunga kuwe kukamu no kuluwala kose, ku soko yo winji wawo.”
11 Hanyuma kanyhi yuyo Malayika wo Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela Hagali yakatya, “Agwe alu wina muda, na kolela mwana wa cilume, nagwe komucemela litagwa lya Ishumayeli. Hambi u Mulungu nha kawuwona u wugaye wako.
12 Nayo yuyo Ishumayeli, kokwikala nhe zindogowe za mu mbago. Nayo kowa mulugu kuli munhu wowose, no munhu wowose kowa mulugu wakwe. Nayo mu mikalile yakwe yose kowa yetemanuce ne wanduguze wose.”

13 Nayo Hagali yakehwesa yakatya, “Mwene mbeka nimuwona Mulungu, na vyo nghali nili mumi du?” Holeka Hagali yakamucemela litagwa u Mulungu mono vyaduwana nayo kuko, yakamutya, “U Mulungu mono kawonaga.”
14 Ayi yo soko yono yaleka lisima lililya, likacemwa Belilahayi-loyi. Ne lisima lilyo lili haciligati ye Kadeshi na Beledi.
15 Ale, Hagali yakamulelela Abulamu mwana wa cilume. Nayo Abulamu yakamucemela mwana yuyo litagwa lya Ishumayeli.
16 Nayo Abulamu yali yaficila myaka makumi munana na mutandatu, lono Hagali yamulelela Ishumayeli.


Mwamulizo 17

Calamo, co citanjilo ce cilagane

1 Mu vilo vyono Abulamu yali yaficila myaka makumi cenda ne 'cenda, u Muwaha Mulungu yakamulawila, yakamutya, “Ane yo nene mono nili Mulungu u Mudaha Vyose. Holeka agwe wutyatye wutonji wangu du, ho wuwe wusina yilema.
2 Nane nonoza cilagane nagwe no kuwinjifya u lulelo lwako.”
3 Baho Abulamu yakafugama, yakenama sunga no wusu wakwe wukapamiza ho hasi. Mulungu yakamutya,
4 “Nghucinoza i cilagane cicino hamonga nagwe, vyono, agwe kowa sogo we makungugo menji.
5 Na ku wulawa lulu, ilitagwa lyako si kocemwa kanyhi Abulamu, langa kocemwa Abulahamu. Hambi ane nha nakondya kukunoza ho wuwe sogo we makungugo menji.
6 Nane nokunoza wuwe na ndelo nyinji muno muno. Kanyhi na mu wulawilila muli gwegwe, noganoza makungugo gawe menji, sunga ne watemi wose, wolawilila muli gwegwe.

7 “Nane nocikangaza i cilagane cangu nagwe, hamonga no lulelo lwako, hamonga ne zindelo zawo zose cibitilila, vyono ane nowa Mulungu wako hamonga ne zindelo zako cibitilila.
8 Nane nokupela gwegwe, hamonga no lulelo lwako, yisi yiyi yono wikaye nha wuli mujenzi, nha cinji yisi yose ya Kanani, yiwe yisi yenyu cibitilila, nane nowa Mulungu wawo.”

9 Hanyuma u Muwaha Mulungu yakamulonjela kanyhi Abulahamu yakatya, “Ku lubavu lwako gwegwe go mweneco, kocibata i cilagane cangu, hamonga no lulelo lwako, ne zindelo zawo zose.
10 Na aci co cilagane cangu cono wunojeye wucibate no kucikamisa, hagati yangu na gwegwe hamonga no lulelo lwako. Nha cinji, u mulume wowose wa muli'nye yimunojeye yalame.
11 Nanye mokwalama, na mu walame wuwo, cowa co citanjilo ce cilagane cangu hagati yangu na nyenye.
12 No mwana wo wose we cilume wa muli nyenye, mono yaficila vilo munana, yimunojeye yalame. No munhu wo wose we cilume wa mu zindelo zenyu zose, hamba yawe muwanda, mono yaleleywe mu zinyumba zenyu, hamba yawe mugulwa na sawo ku wulawa kuli wajenzi,
13 wose wawo yiwanojeye walame. Na yiyi yo yolajila vyono hocili cilagane hagati yangu na nyenye, i cilagane ce cibitilila.
14 No munhu we cilume wo wose mono si yalame, yuyo kokomwa muli wanhu wakwe, vyono kowa yabena i cilagane cangu.”

15 U Mulungu yakamulonjela Abulahamu yakatya, “Kuli za Salayi u mugolece wako, ku wulawa lulu, nayo sikocemwa litagwa lyakwe kanyhi Salayi, aa ng'o, langa mwene kocemwa Sala, nane nomulugutiza.
16 Ninga hamonga na zizo, mwene Sala, kowa yo nyina we makungugo menji, hamonga ne watemi wolawa muli mwene.”
17 Baho Abulahamu, yakafugama, yakenama, sunga no wusu wakwe wukapamiza ho hasi, yakaseka, yakawa kwihwesa mu nhumbula yakwe, “Alu mwene mbeka yosumika mwana yalelwe kuli munhu mukombi nha nene, mono yaficile myaka yigana limonga? Hamba wule, alu mbeka Sala mono yaficila myaka makumi 'cenda, hongo aluno kosuma kutuga mwana?”
18 Hodu Abulahamu yakamulonjela Mulungu yakatya, “Yali yiwe yili ciba le, hono wali wulumbe Ishumayeli yawe muhazi wangu.”
19 Mulungu nayo yakamutya, “Aa, ng'o Abulahamu! Langa Sala u mugolece wako yo kokulelela mwana we cilume, nagwe komucemela litagwa lya Isaka. Nane, muli mwene Isaka, mo nocikangaziza i cilagane cangu hamonga no lulelo lwakwe, ho ciwe cilagane ca cibitilila.

20 “Na kuli za Ishumayeli, nakondya kuwuhulika u wulombe wako. Holeka nayo nomulugutiza, nane nomupela wana wenji, no kuwenjifya muno muno. Nayo Ishumayeli kowa sogo we watemi yikumi na wejete, nane nomunoza yawe yikungugo yiwaha.
21 Ninga i cilagane cangu aco langa nocikangaziza muli Isaka du, mono Sala kokulelela nha mu madaha gagano ku mwaka.”
22 U Mulungu yali yeza kukondya kuduwana na Abulahamu, hodu yakawa kuwuka.
23 Nayo Abulahamu yakawa kuwakonghola hamonga, Ishumayeli mwanagwe, ne wawanda wose we cilume walelelwa mu nyumba yakwe, hamonga na wono wagulwa ne sawo yakwe. Yakawa kuwategula wose ku calamo mwi'zuwa lilyo, nha vyono Mulungu yamulajiliza.
24 Nayo Abulahamu mweneco, yalama lono yali yaficila myaka makumi yicenda ne 'cenda.
25 No mwanagwe Ishumayeli, nayo yalama lono yali yaficila myaka yikumi na midatu.
26 I lizuwa lilyo lyo walamila hamonga Abulahamu na Ishumayeli mwanagwe.
27 Na we cilume wose wa mu nyumba yakwe, ne wawanda wose walelelwa mumo mu nyumba yakwe, na wose i wagulwa ne sawo yakwe ku wulawa kuli wajenzi, wose wakalama mwi'zuwa lilyo hamonga na Abulahamu.


Mwamulizo 18

Abulahamu yakwihuwizizwa kutuga mwana

1 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakamulawila Abulahamu lono yali yali ha mibici ye minghola, ya Mamule. Mwene lulo Abulahamu yali yekaye ha mulango we lihema lyakwe mwi'pango lye livuce mu misi mugati.
2 Nayo yali yeza kwinula i meso gakwe, yakawawona wanhu wadatu wemile. Nayo yali yeza kuwawona, yakamangha mbela mbela, yakabita kuwabocela. Yakafugama ha mwande yawo, yakenama sunga no wusu wakwe wukapamiza ho hasi, yakatya,
3 “Nye nyo walumbwe, hono niwe natuga wuwonelwa lusungu na nyenye, ale mulece kumhulutiza, ane nili baha ho nimwitumamilenyi.
4 Nane nomuletelenyi malenga madodo mwizuguze migulu yenyu, mucehe hadodo ahano hasi yo mubici.
5 Ne cipango cono mowa mukali mucehile, nane nomuwikanizizenyi ndigwa ndodo mulye, ho mwiwikanize so muhinzo wenyu, vyono munyiziza ane, holeka mulece nimwitumamilenyi.” Nawo wakamutya, “Ale du, witumaje nha vyono wulonjile.”

6 Nayo Abulahamu yakawuya mbela mbela kwi'hema lyakwe, yakawa kumulonjela mugolece wakwe Sala yakutya, “Ale, wuwikanize mbela mbela vipimo vidatu vyo wusaje wuswanu, wunoze mikate.”
7 Hanyuma mwene Abulahamu, yakawa kwinjila musunho mwi'tewa lye zing'ombe, yakawa kuyihagula ndama monga swanu muno muno yono yijinile, yakawa kumupela mwitumami, nayo yakawa kuyiteleka mbela mbela.
8 Abulahamu nayo yakasola tilazi, mele, hamonga na yiyo nyama, yakawatenjela wawo wajenzi ho walye ndigwa, nayo yakema behi nawo ho yawetumamile, lono walizaga baho hasi yo mubici.
9 I wajenzi nawo wakamuwuza wakatya, “Alu Sala u mugolece wako yali hayi?” Nayo Abulahamu yakawapitula yakatya, “Sala yali mwi'hema.”
10 Yunji muyawo yakatya, “Ane nokuwuyila kanyhi ku mwaka, nha mu madaha gagano, no mugolece wako Sala, kowa yena mwana wa cilume.” Kumbe nayo Sala yakawa yemile ha mulango we lihema kwa kugati yayo yahulicize.
11 Nawo wa Abulahamu na mwene Sala, wali weza wakombi, nawo wali wakondya kuficila mu mazika mawaha muno. Nayo Sala i vilo vyakwe vyo mwezi vyali vyakondya kuduma.
12 Holeka Sala yali yeza kuzihulika zizo, yakaseka, yakawa kwihwesa mu nhumbula yakwe yakutya, “Alu ane nili mukombi, no mulume wangu nhavivilya nayo mukombi. Alu mbeka yosumika hongo, nane sangalale hamonga no mulume wangu?”
13 Baho u Muwaha Mulungu yakamuwuza Abulahamu yakatya, “Alu ku sokoci Sala kaseka, no kwiwuza nha vyono yosumika mbeka kuli mwene yatuje mwana na lono yeza mukombi?
14 Alu wule, wukwihwesa hoyili nghani yoyose yono siyisumice hali Muwaha Mulungu? Ale, ane nokuwuyila kanyhi ku mwaka kwinha vivi, no mugolece wako Sala kowa na mwana wa cilume.”
15 Ninga Sala vyono yali yenjilwa na woga wuwaha, holeka yakakana yakutya, “Ane sisecile yaye!” Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakamutya, “Gwe Sala, wulece kukana! Mbeka kuseka.”

Abulahamu yakulitetela likaya lya Sodoma

16 Ale, i wanhu wawalya i wadatu, wakawa kuwuka wakawa kubita hanhu hono henucile, hono wosuma kuliwona viswanu likaya lya Sodoma, nayo Abulahamu yakawa kuwasindiciza.
17 U Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Si yowa viswanu hono nimuhundice Abulahamu, zinghani zono nohila kuzituma mu vilo vivi.
18 Nomunoza mwene Abulahamu yawe yikungugo yiwaha lye vilungo, na muli mwene, i makungugo gose ga mononze golugutizwa.
19 Nimuhagula ho yawejize wanagwe hamonga no lulelo lwakwe, wayibate i nzila yangu nene no Muwaha Mulungu, mu wituma ziswanu na zo wiling'ano, nane no Muwaha Mulungu ho nimufisilizize zono namwihuwiza.”

20 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakajendelela kanyhi kulonga yakatya, “Kuko ku Sodoma na Gomola, kowuli wung'ung'ulice winji muno muno, ku soko yo wusutye wawo vyono wuwaha muno.
21 Holeka nane nobita kuko, ho nghewonelele no mweneco, hono mbeka wakwituma nha vyono genha i mang'ung'uliko gono nghugahulika kuli wene, na hono yiwe vyo si yinha, ale nomanya.”
22 Na ku wulawilila baho, wanhu wawalya wakawa kutuluka no kubita ku Sodoma. Ninga Abulahamu, yakasigala yemile du hali Mulungu.
23 Hanyuma Abulahamu yakasejelela hali Mulungu, yakatya, “Alu mwene mbeka du kosuma kuwongomeza i wanhu waswanu hamonga ne wanhu wabi?
24 Na lunji yisumice wawe mowali wanhu waswanu makumi mahano mumo mwi'kaya, alu wule, kosuma kulyongomeza likaya hongo, hamba si kolileka yaye ku soko ye wanhu wawo we makumi mahano wono wali ling'ani?
25 Ninga nitumbiye vyono si konoza nhavivyo! Nha cinji wuwawulaje i wanhu waswanu hamonga ne wanhu wabi, wo wutya, i waswanu wanozezwe muling'ano ne wanhu wabi! Aa ng'o! Viswanu kuli muling'anizi we yinze yose kuwituma ling'ani.”
26 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakamupitula Abulahamu yakamutya, “Hono niwagane wanhu waswanu makumi mahano kuko ku Sodoma, hodu nolileka likaya yitelevu ku soko ya wawo.”

27 Nayo Abulahamu yakatya, “Ane mono sinojeye, kanyhi si nghwiliye kulongana no Mulungu, hambi ane nha nili manghundi na mavu du!
28 Na lunji yisumice wawe mowali wanhu waswanu wali makumi mane na wahano du, wono si waficile makumi mahano. Wule, kolyongomeza likaya lyose ku soko vyono wacepuce wanhu wahano?” Nayo Mulungu yakamupitula yakatya, “Aa, ng'o! Sinolyongomeza likaya hono wawe mowali wanhu waswanu makumi mane na wahano.”
29 Abulahamu nayo kanyhi yakatya, “Na lunji wowa wanhu waswanu makumi mane du wono wowa wali mumo.” U Muwaha Mulungu nayo yakapitula yakatya, “Ku soko ye wanhu wawo makumi mane i waswanu, sinonoza nhavivyo yaye.”
30 Nayo Abulahamu yakacila du kulonga, yakatya, “Gwe Muwaha, nghukunena wulece kusimba, ane nghunena nonje kanyhi. Alu wule, ma, yisolaga lunji wejele mumo wanhu waswanu makumi madatu du.” Nayo Mulungu yakatya, “Sinonoza nhavivyo hono niwagane mumo wawo we makumi madatu.”

31 Abulahamu kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe Muwaha, nghukunena wunenele ane mono sinojeye hali'gwe, njendelele kulongana nagwe. Na lunji wokwijela mumo makumi mejete ma?” U Muwaha Mulungu nayo yakatya, “Sinosuma kulyongomeza likaya, hono niwagane mumo makumi mejete.”
32 Kanyhi Abulahamu yakatya, “Nghukunena Muwaha, wulece kukolwa ne likuwo, nane nolongana nagwe nghanghulo monga du. Ma na lunji wokwijela wali 'kumi du?” Nayo Mulungu yakatya, “Sinosuma kulyongomeza likaya, hono wejele mumo wali kumi du.”
33 Nayo Mulungu yali yeza kukondya kulongana na Abulahamu, hodu yakawa kubita, nayo Abulahamu yakawa kuwuya ku kaya kwakwe.


Mwamulizo 19

U wubi we wanhu wa ku Sodoma

1 I wamalayika wa walya wejete, lono waziza ku Sodoma, nayo Loti mwi'daha lilyo yali yekaye he'lango lye likaya lya Sodoma. Nayo Loti yali yeza kuwawona, yakenuka yakawahinjiliza, yakagundama u wusu wakwe ko kuwalamusa,
2 yakatya, “Nye nyo walumbwe, alenyi muhelele mu nyumba yangu ane no mwitumami wenyu. Mugone bahano hono hangu mu cilo cicino, ho musume kwizuguza i migulu yenyu. Nanye mokwilawa kwinuka hakali mitondo muno muno no kujendelela no muhinzo wenyu.” Nawo wakamutya, “Ng'o! Ase langa cogona kukuno du kono ciwanza sunga wele.”
3 Nayo Loti yakawanena muno muno, nhondo nawo wakavumila, wakabitana nayo ko ku kaya yakwe. Nayo Loti yakawanozeza ndigwa nyinji, za sikukuu, yakawanozeza ne mikate yono yisina cisase, nawo wakayilya.

4 Ninga lono wali weza wali behi no kugona, i walume wose wa mu Sodoma, i wazelelo hamonga ne wakombi wose, hamba yunji kusigala, wakaza wakayipilima kaya yiyilya ya Loti.
5 Wakawa kwanza kumucema Loti akuno wakumuwuza wakutya, “I wanhu wa walya we cilume wono wazize kugona mu kaya yako, wali'hayi wene? Ale walavyaje lulu baha kono'nze cigone nawo.”
6 Loti yakawa kulawila kunze, akuno yakuwudinda no mulango cinyamugongo,
7 yakawalonjela yakatya, “Nye wandugu zangu, ane nghumunenenyi muno muno, mulece kunoza nghani yiyi 'mbi.
8 Ninga mulanje ta, ane nina wanangu wahinza wanyacipale wejete, wono wakali hamba si namumanya mulume yaye. Holeka mulece nimupelenyi wawo wahinza, nanye muwatendele nha vyono mowona viswanu nyenye. Ninga nghumunenenyi dedede, mulece kuwatendela nghani yo yose 'mbi wanhu wawa, hambi wene nha wajenzi wangu nene.”

9 Ninga wene wakatya, “Wuka ko gwe! Agwe waza bahano nha wuli mujenzi, aluno weza kwinoza hongo nha wuli hakimu! Alu ase cokunoza vibi muno muno si nhe wajenzi wako wawo.” Hodu baho baho du, wakawa kumukundujiziza Loti ku nyuma, hanga wawubene no mulango wakwe.
10 Ninga wawalya wajenzi wakamukwejela Loti mu nyumba kugati, no kuwudinda mulango.
11 Wawo wajenzi, wakawatunduwaza meso i wanhu wawo wose i wadodo ne wawaha wono wali wali ha mulango. Holeka vyono meso gawo gali gatunduwala, wakawulondola u mulango hamba kuwuwona yaye.

U wuwuce wa Loti ku Sodoma

12 Wanhu wawalya i wejete wakamulonjela Loti wakamutya, “Alu wule, kowa wina munhu yunji wo wose bahano, lunji wanago, we cilume na we cicekulu, hamba lunji wanji wa mu mhuga yako wono wekaye mwi'kaya lilino? Hono yiwe vyo yinha, ale, walavyaje wawuce bahano musunho muno muno.
13 Vyono ase ceza cili behi no kulyongomeza likaya lili. Hambi i cililo ce wekazi wa baha cono cakondya kumuficila u Muwaha Mulungu, nha ciwaha muno, alu nayo Mulungu kacituma ase ho cize cilyongomeze.”
14 Hodu Loti yakawa kuwabitila i wazelelo wono wasakaga kuwatola i wahinza wakwe, yakawatya, “Ale mulawile muwuce bahano mbela mbela! Hambi u Muwaha Mulungu nha kolyongomeza likaya lili lulu baha.” Ninga wene wakamuwona nha munhu mono yakwizawa du.
15 Waza kwela, wamalayika wakamuswaswaniza Loti muno muno, akuno wakutya, “Gwe Loti, Loti! Winukaje ye, musolaje no mugolece wako, hamonga ne wanago wejete we cicekulu, mulece kwajiliza wumi wenyu bwete, kolo likaya lilino likongomezwa.”
16 Ninga mwene Loti yakawa yakwipilimiza pilimiza du. Ninga vyono u Mulungu yamuwonela muno lusungu Loti, wamalayika wawalya wakamwibata muwoko, mwene no mugolece wakwe hamonga ne wanagwe wawalya i wejete we cicekulu, no kuwategula ko kunze ye likaya lya Sodoma.
17 Nawo wali weza kuwafisa kunze ye likaya, yunji muyagwe ne wamalayika wawo, yakawatya, “Ale, mutize, mwiponye u wumi wenyu. Ninga mulanjilile! Mulecele lukulu kwima kwima hanhu ho hose mwi'lolo hamba kwilenga ku nyuma. Mutizize mu matunda, ho mulece kongomela.”

18 Mwene Loti yakawatya wamalayika, “Nye mo Walumbwe!
19 Heya yili mbeka anye mumbonela lusungu ane no mwitumami wenyu, na mbeka munhendela viswanu muno muno, so kuwuponya u wumi wangu, ninga alu kuko mu matunda nha kutali muno muno. Ku soko vyono nghulanga, u wongomezwe wuwo nha wonyagana lono nghali hamba sinafika kuko, holeka nofwa du.
20 Ninga lanjenyi ta akulya ku nhumbi, kocili cikaya cidodo cono nosuma kutiziza kuko vyono ho behi na baha. Holeka nghumunenenyi munghombole ho nhizize kuko. Vyono ku cikaya cicilya cidodo, nane kuko ko noponela.”
21 Nayo malayika yakamupitula yakamutya, “Ale, viswanu! Nane nawuvumila wuhile wako. Holeka nane sinocongomeza yaye, i cikaya cico cono wulonjile.
22 Ninga, le, wiyohaje mbela mbela. Wutizize kuko, nane sinonoza cinji co cose, sunga kolo wakondya kufika ku cikaya cico.” Holeka i cikaya cico cikacemwa Sowali, vyono Loti mweneco yacitya cikaya cidodo.

U wongomezwe we makaya ga Sodoma na Gomola

23 I lizuwa lyali lyasosomoka lono Loti yaficila ku cikaya ca Sowali.
24 Baho u Mulungu nayo yakawa kutonyeza cibiliti no moto mu Sodoma na Gomola ku wulawa ku wulanga.
25 Yakagongomeza i makaya gago ne lilolo lyose, hamonga na wose wono wekalaga mumo, sunga ne vilimwa vyose vya mu yisi yiyo.
26 Ninga u mugolece wa Loti vyono yajendelaga mu nyuma ya Loti, woyi he! Mwene yakapituka yakelenga ku nyuma, na hobaho baho du, yakawa kuhunduka, yakawa lisumbili lya mwino.
27 Hakali mitondo muno muno, Abulahamu yakabita hanhu hono vyaduwana no Mulungu.
28 Nayo lono yali yemile baho, yaza kulanga lubavu lwa ku Sodoma na Gomola na ku lubavu lose lwe lilolo, yakezina muno muno kuwona lyosi lyono lyabitaga kucanyha, lyosi lyono linha lye nyanyilo yiwaha.
29 U Muwaha Mulungu lono yagongomezeza i makaya ga Sodoma na Gomola, gono gali gali mwi'lolo kono yekalaga Loti, Mulungu yamukumbucila Abulahamu ko kumulavyaniza Loti ku wulawa mu makaya gago, Loti ho yalece kongomezwa hamonga ne makaya gago.

U mwamulizo we Wamowabi ne Waamoni

30 Ninga Loti yakogopa ku wikala ku Sowali, holeka yakahama kuko, mwene hamonga ne wanagwe wejete we cicekulu, wakabita kwikala mu mapango mu matunda.
31 Nawo lono wali wali kuko, u mwanagwe u muhinza muwaha, yakamulonjela u mwanakwawe yakatya, “Alu u sogo yetu yeza kombipa, na hasina mulume mono'nze mono kocitola ho cilele wana.
32 Holeka ane nghuwona, yowa viswanu hono cimupele sogo yetu divayi, yang'we, yagale, cisume kugona nayo, ho cituje lulelo kuli mwene.”
33 Ale, mu cilo cico wakamupela sogo yawo divayi, yakang'wa sunga yakakolwa muno muno. Ale, u muhinza muwaha, yakagona no sogwe. Ninga u sogwe si yamanya hamba hadodo, hambi nha yali yakoligwe muno divayi.

34 I mitondo yaco, u mwana we cicekulu muwaha, yakawa kumulonjela u mwanakwawe yakutya, “Wayiko mwanakwetu, ane no muyago, yigolo ne cilo, nagona na baba, holeka izuwali cimupelaje kanyhi divayi, yang'we yakolwe, hanyuma nagwe ho wubite wukagone nayo, ho cituje lulelo ko sogo yetu.”
35 Ale, mu cilo cico wakamupela kanyhi sogo yawo divayi, nayo yakang'wa sunga yakakolwa. U mwana we cicekulu u mudodo, mu cilo cico nayo yakabita yakagona no sogwe. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, u sogwe si yamanya hamba hadodo, hambi nha yali yakoligwe.
36 Hodu, i wana wawo wa Loti wose vyono wejete wakawa watuga mida, ku wulawilila ko sogo yawo.
37 Ayulya u mwana muwaha yakasawa mwana wa cilume, yakamucemela litagwa lya Mowabu. Yuyo yo sogo ye Wamowabi sunga nalu.
38 Nayo u mwanakwawe yakasawa mwana wa cilume, nayo yakamucemela litagwa lya Benami. Nayo yuyo yo sogo ye Waamoni sunga nalu.


Mwamulizo 20

Abulahamu na Abimeleki

1 Abulahamu yakatuluka yakulawa ku Mamule, yakawa kubita ku yisi ya Negebu, nayo yakekala haciligati ya Kadeshi na Shuli, kanyhi yakekala hadodo na ku Gelali.
2 Nayo Abulahamu lono yali yali kuko, yakenoza u mugolece wakwe Sala kotya nha lumbulye. Holeka u mutemi Abimeleki wa ku Gelali yakamusola Sala.
3 Ninga Mulungu yakamubitila Abimeleki ne cilo mu ndoto, yakamutya, “Langa ta, agwe lu kofwa, ku soko yo kumusola yuyo mucekulu, hambi nha mugolece wa munhu yunji.”
4 Ninga hamba nhavivyo Abimeleki yali yakali si nagona na Sala ng'o. Holeka nayo Abimeleki yakatya, “Alu Muwaha, ho kowawulaga sunga ne wanhu wono wasina masutya!
5 Alu si yo mweneco Abulahamu mono yenoza yeze Sala nha lumbulye? Kanyhi hamba nhavivyo, Sala nayo yenoza Abulahamu nha lumbulye! Ane nane nayinoza nghani yiyi ko mutima muzelu muno muno, holeka sina yisutya.”

6 Nayo Mulungu yakamulonjela Abimeleki mu ndoto yakatya, “Ale, heya viswanu! Nane manyile nha vyono wayituma nghani yiyi ko mutima muzelu, ane nane nha yo nene mono nghwimiye ho wulece kwituma wubi kuli'ne, holeka singhukomboye ho wugone no mucekulu yuyu.
7 Ale, hodu aluno, wumugomolaje u mucekulu yuyu ko mulume wakwe, mwene yuyo muwizi, nayo kokulombela ho wulece kufwa. Ninga hono wulece kumugomola, ale wumanyile lukulu vyono agwe kofwa, gwegwe go mweneco hamonga ne wanhu wako wose.”
8 Hodu, Abimeleki yakapinjila kwinuka hakali mitondo muno muno, yakawacema i watazi wakwe wose, yakawaloloseza zose zono yaziwene mu ndoto, nawo wakenjilwa na woga muno muno.
9 Nayo Abimeleki yakamucema Abulahamu, yakamuwuza yakatya, “Alu Abulahamu, ho kucitendela vye wule? Alu ane nghusutizya cici, sunga kuwa kundetela zimbali zizino hamonga no wutemi wangu? Mbeka kunhendela nghani zono si zikwiliye hamba hadodo.”
10 Abimeleki yakacila kumuwuza kanyhi, yakatya, “Mwene agwe Abulahamu, alu mwene wali kwihwesa nhawule, sunga kuwa kwituma zono zinha zizi?”
11 Nayo Abulahamu yakamupitula yakatya, “Ane nituma nhavivyo, vyono nali kwihwesa hasina munhu wo wose bahano mono yamogopilo Mulungu. Na kanyhi ane, nali kogopa nghutya lunji mombulaga, mumusole mugolece wangu.
